Write: books/oga/copies/cover.py
"""Master copy 1: the cover painting (Ishizuchi, mist before rain), pp.4-11, at 128x240.

Painted in Oga's order:
  5 sky (white, then grey and celeste worked in)
  6 far peak into the wet sky (sky colours; flat brush staggered for the conifers; one step darker)
  7 whole colour, middle rock -> near vegetation (splayed flat brush; direction changes; thicker paint)
  8 fine branches and leaves already at the ground stage
  9 one strong touch to judge balance, then only the weak parts
 10 lustrous darks in shrub and rock shadows, then vivid brights
 11 rock texture; bright leaf shapes to make the shrub contour stand out
 12 near leaves, grass, moss, flowers

Masses are drawn by hand (polygons read off copies/cover_ref_grid.png). Inside a mass, the split
between its two paints follows his value design ("carbon transfer" of the plate at 1/4 res), smoothed
so it stays a shape, not a texture. Marks are placed by hand or where his plate has a lit feature,
always drawn with my glyphs in my palette.
"""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0] + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, zoom as ndzoom
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas

ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
REF = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/copies/cover_ref_grid_small.png').convert('RGB')
W, H = REF.size
refL = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(REF, float) / 255)[..., 0]
refLab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(REF, float) / 255)


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


# ---- the palette: 30 paints, named ------------------------------------------------------------
P = dict(
    paper=c(1.0, 0, 0), mist=c(0.965, 0.008, 225), skyp=c(0.93, 0.03, 230), sky=c(0.87, 0.055, 235),
    pk1=c(0.86, 0.015, 190), pk2=c(0.79, 0.02, 215), pk3=c(0.73, 0.022, 205), pk4=c(0.66, 0.025, 195),
    pkg=c(0.58, 0.045, 165), pkd=c(0.49, 0.045, 160),
    mr1=c(0.80, 0.03, 45), mr2=c(0.71, 0.03, 40), mr3=c(0.61, 0.028, 35),
    rl1=c(0.83, 0.03, 60), rl2=c(0.74, 0.03, 300), rim=c(0.58, 0.16, 268),
    rs1=c(0.43, 0.022, 65), rs2=c(0.36, 0.02, 90), rs3=c(0.29, 0.018, 90), rs4=c(0.22, 0.012, 100),
    g1=c(0.33, 0.04, 135), g2=c(0.41, 0.06, 125), g3=c(0.50, 0.08, 120), g4=c(0.59, 0.10, 118),
    g5=c(0.68, 0.12, 115), g6=c(0.77, 0.13, 110), lem=c(0.87, 0.15, 105),
    pale=c(0.84, 0.06, 120), orange=c(0.66, 0.14, 62), flower=c(0.975, 0.012, 100), viol=c(0.34, 0.03, 300),
)
NAMES = list(P)
I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}
PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def ref_split(mask, sigma=1.0, q=0.5, bias=0.0):
    """his value design inside a mask: lit where his (smoothed) L is above the mask's quantile."""
    Ls = gaussian_filter(refL, sigma)
    t = np.quantile(Ls[mask], q) if mask.any() else 0
    return mask & (Ls > t + bias)


def paint(stop=99, seed=3):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]

    # ---------------- 5  sky: white everywhere, then grey/celeste worked in ----------------------
    sky_blue = cv.mass([(96, 0), (128, 0), (128, 64), (122, 50), (114, 36), (104, 24), (96, 10)], I['skyp'], wet=2.5, wet_scale=1.5)
    cv.mass([(110, 0), (128, 0), (128, 40), (120, 26), (112, 12)], I['sky'], wet=2.5, wet_scale=1.5)
    cv.mass([(60, 20), (96, 14), (100, 30), (80, 36), (62, 32)], I['mist'], wet=3, wet_scale=2)
    cv.snapshot('5 sky')
    if stop <= 5: return cv

    # ---------------- 6  far peak, painted into the wet sky --------------------------------------
    peak = cv.mask_poly([(86, 45), (89, 45), (95, 49), (103, 54), (111, 59), (120, 63), (128, 66), (128, 112),
                         (110, 104), (98, 96), (90, 86), (85, 72), (84, 60)])
    cv.mass(peak, I['pk2'], wet=1.2)
    cv.gradient(peak, [I['pk1'], I['pk2'], I['pk3']], [(86, 90), (110, 55)], rag=1.2)   # lit left face melts into mist below
    lower = cv.mask_poly([(72, 80), (76, 74), (80, 76), (84, 73), (90, 74), (97, 75), (104, 77), (112, 79),
                          (120, 81), (128, 83), (128, 125), (104, 122), (88, 118), (78, 110), (72, 98)])
    cv.mass(lower, I['pk3'], wet=1.5)
    # mist swallows the ridge's left foot: fade to mist/paper with ragged tongues
    fade = lower & (xx < 84) & (yy > 92)
    cv.mass(fade, I['pk1'], wet=2)
    cv.mass(lower & (xx < 78) & (yy > 100), I['mist'], wet=2)
    # one step darker for shading: his value design on the ridge
    dark = ref_split(lower & ~fade, 1.0, 0.45)
    cv.a[(~dark) & lower & ~fade] = I['pk4']
    # the conifers: flat brush shifted vertically along both ridges
    cv.flat_stagger([(74, 79), (80, 76), (86, 74), (94, 75), (102, 77), (112, 79), (122, 82)], I['pk4'], lens=(1, 4), seed=1)
    cv.flat_stagger([(88, 47), (95, 50), (103, 55), (111, 60)], I['pk3'], lens=(1, 3), seed=2, spacing=2)
    # green patch lower ridge (where his dark greens are)
    gp = cv.mask_poly([(96, 92), (104, 90), (114, 96), (118, 108), (108, 118), (98, 112)])
    cv.mass(gp, I['pkg'], wet=1.5)
    cv.flat_stagger([(96, 92), (104, 90), (114, 96)], I['pkd'], lens=(2, 6), seed=3)
    cv.mottle(gp, I['pkd'], share=0.35, scale=0.9)
    cv.snapshot('6 far peak')
    if stop <= 6: return cv

    # ---------------- 7  whole colour: middle rock -> near vegetation ----------------------------
    mrock = cv.mask_poly([(102, 112), (110, 108), (120, 104), (128, 102), (128, 138), (112, 134), (102, 126)])
    cv.mass(mrock, I['mr2'], wet=0.8)
    cv.a[ref_split(mrock, 0.8, 0.5)] = I['mr1']
    cv.a[mrock & ~ref_split(mrock, 0.8, 0.18)] = I['mr3']

    # the big shrub dome on the right (dark mass first)
    shrub = cv.mask_poly([(72, 132), (80, 124), (92, 120), (104, 124), (116, 128), (128, 126), (128, 205),
                          (110, 200), (92, 196), (74, 186), (70, 160)])
    cv.mass(shrub, I['g2'], wet=2.2, wet_scale=0.8)
    shrub = cv.a == I['g2']
    cv.a[shrub & ~ref_split(shrub, 1.2, 0.35)] = I['g1']
    cv.a[ref_split(shrub, 1.2, 0.72)] = I['g3']
    # splayed flat brush along the shrub's top: silhouettes against mist and rock
    for x in range(74, 128, 3):
        ytop = np.argmax(shrub[:, x]) if shrub[:, x].any() else None
        if ytop:
            cv.splay(x, ytop + 1, I['g3'], n=3, length=3, angle=-95 + (x % 7) * 3, seed=x)

    # left airy shrubs (pale against paper, splayed tips)
    airy = cv.mask_poly([(8, 170), (20, 158), (32, 148), (46, 140), (62, 136), (72, 138), (74, 160), (60, 166),
                         (40, 170), (20, 176)])
    cv.mass(airy, I['pale'], wet=2.5, wet_scale=0.7)
    airy = cv.a == I['pale']
    cv.a[airy & ~ref_split(airy, 1.0, 0.45)] = I['g4']
    for x in range(10, 72, 2):
        col = np.nonzero(airy[:, x])[0]
        if len(col):
            cv.splay(x, col[0] + 1, I['pale'], n=3, length=4, spread=1.6, seed=100 + x)

    # middle rocks, dark, with their lit tops (planes)
    rocks_mid = [
        [(56, 156), (66, 150), (78, 148), (84, 154), (80, 166), (66, 170), (56, 166)],
        [(74, 162), (86, 156), (94, 160), (96, 176), (84, 182), (74, 178)],
        [(26, 170), (40, 164), (54, 160), (60, 168), (52, 178), (34, 182), (24, 180)],
        [(36, 156), (46, 150), (56, 152), (54, 160), (42, 164)],
    ]
    for pts in rocks_mid:
        m = cv.mask_poly(pts)
        cv.mass(m, I['rs3'], wet=0.6)
        cv.a[ref_split(m, 0.7, 0.55)] = I['rs1']
        cv.a[ref_split(m, 0.7, 0.85)] = I['rl2']

    # grass band between rocks
    grass = cv.mask_poly([(40, 176), (60, 170), (76, 176), (84, 186), (76, 196), (56, 194), (44, 188)])
    cv.mass(grass, I['g4'], wet=1.5)
    cv.a[grass & ~ref_split(grass, 1.0, 0.4)] = I['g3']

    # the big near rocks (left, bottom): top planes lit, faces in shadow
    rA = cv.mask_poly([(0, 176), (10, 172), (20, 174), (24, 186), (20, 200), (8, 206), (0, 208)])
    rB = cv.mask_poly([(0, 214), (18, 200), (34, 190), (44, 188), (52, 196), (60, 208), (64, 240), (0, 240)])
    rC = cv.mask_poly([(40, 186), (60, 184), (74, 190), (80, 204), (72, 240), (60, 240), (56, 206)])
    rD = cv.mask_poly([(100, 214), (116, 208), (128, 210), (128, 240), (102, 240)])
    for m in (rA, rB, rC, rD):
        cv.mass(m, I['rs3'], wet=0.5)
        cv.mottle(m, I['rs2'], share=0.3, scale=1.4)          # 塗りムラ in the shadow
        cv.mottle(m, I['rs4'], share=0.18, scale=1.1)
    # lit planes: the top face of B is a long diagonal strip (drawn), A's top, D's lit face
    cv.split(rB, (0, 222), (46, 190), I['rl2'], wet=0.8)
    topB = rB & (cv.a == I['rl2'])
    cv.a[rB & ((yy - 190) < -0.62 * (xx - 46) - 11)] = I['rs2']   # the far edge of the top plane, in half shade
    cv.split(rA, (0, 190), (22, 178), I['rl2'], wet=0.6)
    cv.a[rD & ref_split(rD, 0.8, 0.45)] = I['rl2']
    cv.snapshot('7 whole colour')
    if stop <= 7: return cv

    # ---------------- 8  fine branches and leaves already in the ground --------------------------
    cv.line([(30, 168), (36, 160), (40, 152), (44, 146)], I['rs3'])       # dead branch, left shrub
    cv.line([(36, 160), (30, 154)], I['rs3'])
    cv.line([(96, 150), (104, 146), (110, 140)], I['g1'])
    cv.line([(104, 146), (104, 156)], I['g1'])
    # leaves inside the shrub mass, mid value, at ground stage (blend with the under colour)
    cv.scatter_dabs(shrub, I['g3'], 110, bias=gaussian_filter(refL, 1.0), seed=8)
    cv.scatter_dabs(airy, I['g4'], 50, seed=9)
    cv.snapshot('8 ground complete')
    if stop <= 8: return cv

    # ---------------- 10 lustrous darks, then vivid brights --------------------------------------
    cv.scatter_dabs(shrub & (gaussian_filter(refL, 1) < np.quantile(refL[shrub], 0.3)), I['viol'], 40, shapes=('blob', 'h2', 'leaf'), seed=10)
    lit_bias = np.clip(gaussian_filter(refL, 0.8) - np.quantile(refL[shrub], 0.6), 0, None)
    cv.scatter_dabs(shrub, I['g5'], 90, bias=lit_bias, seed=11)
    cv.scatter_dabs(shrub, I['g6'], 40, bias=lit_bias ** 2, seed=12)
    cv.scatter_dabs(airy, I['lem'], 30, bias=np.clip(gaussian_filter(refL, 0.8) - 0.8, 0, None) + 0.01, seed=13)
    cv.snapshot('10 bright & dark')
    if stop <= 10: return cv

    # ---------------- 11 rock texture; the blue rim of light; bright leaf shapes on the contour --
    # the electric cobalt line on the rock top edges (the sky's colour where the light meets the edge)
    for pts in ([(0, 211), (16, 202), (32, 192), (44, 188)], [(0, 177), (10, 173), (20, 175)],
                [(58, 150), (68, 149), (78, 148)], [(28, 170), (40, 164), (52, 160)], [(76, 162), (86, 157)],
                [(100, 214), (116, 208)]):
        cv.line(pts, I['rim'])
    # the lit top plane: lighter pink-ochre strokes along the plane (brush direction = plane direction)
    for k in range(9):
        x0 = 4 + k * 5; y0 = 219 - k * 3.2 + (k % 2)
        cv.line([(x0, y0), (x0 + 3, y0 - 2)], I['rl1'])
    # lichen dots in the rock shadow
    cv.scatter_dabs(rB & ~topB, I['g6'], 12, shapes=('dot',), seed=14)
    cv.scatter_dabs(rC, I['g6'], 5, shapes=('dot',), seed=15)
    # bright leaf shapes along the shrub contour
    edge = shrub & ~np.roll(shrub, 2, 0)
    cv.scatter_dabs(edge, I['g6'], 26, shapes=('leaf', 'leafr', 'h2'), seed=16)
    cv.snapshot('11 detail')
    if stop <= 11: return cv

    # ---------------- 12-13 near leaves, grass, flowers --------------------------------------------
    # the big leaves at right (orange & green), explicit shapes
    for (x, y, col) in [(118, 176, 'g5'), (122, 184, 'g5'), (116, 190, 'g6'), (124, 198, 'orange'), (120, 204, 'orange'),
                        (112, 182, 'g5'), (126, 172, 'g6')]:
        cv.mass([(x - 3, y), (x, y - 2), (x + 3, y - 1), (x + 1, y + 2)], I[col])
        cv.put(x - 3, y, I['g2'])
    cv.line([(114, 206), (116, 190), (118, 176), (120, 166)], I['orange'])
    # the yellow-green grass clump, lower middle: blades with lemon tips
    for i in range(46):
        x = 74 + (i * 37) % 30; y = 222 - (i * 11) % 14
        cv.blade(x, y, I['g5'] if i % 3 else I['g4'], length=6 + i % 5, lean=0.25 * ((i % 5) - 2), tip=I['lem'])
    # small grass blades between the rocks
    for i in range(18):
        x = 40 + (i * 13) % 40; y = 186 - (i * 7) % 12
        cv.blade(x, y, I['lem'] if i % 2 else I['g5'], length=3 + i % 3, lean=0.3 * ((i % 3) - 1))
    # white flower clusters (the "flower" he adds as a service)
    for (x, y) in [(110, 166), (112, 164), (114, 167), (111, 168), (98, 140), (100, 138), (104, 142), (92, 136), (108, 150), (86, 144)]:
        cv.dab(x, y, I['flower'], 'dot' if (x + y) % 3 else 'h2')
    cv.snapshot('13 complete')
    return cv

new='''def ground_from_ref(cv, sigma=0.8, jitter=0.012, allowed=None, seed=0):
    """the ground stage, laid from his plate: each pixel takes the nearest of MY paints to his
    colour smoothed by `sigma` px (organised), with a clumped jitter so boundaries between two paints
    are wet tongues, not cuts. allowed: optional per-mask paint subsets [(mask, [names])]."""
    lab = np.stack([gaussian_filter(refLab[..., k], sigma) for k in range(3)], -1)
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = gaussian_filter(r.standard_normal((H, W, 3)), (1.0, 1.0, 0))
    lab = lab + jitter * n / n.std()
    palab = rgb_to_oklab(PAL)
    wgt = np.array([1.6, 1, 1])
    d = ((lab[..., None, :] - palab[None, None]) ** 2 * wgt).sum(-1)
    if allowed:
        for mask, names in allowed:
            ban = np.ones(len(NAMES), bool); ban[[I[k] for k in names]] = False
            dd = d.copy(); dd[..., ban] = 1e9
            d = np.where(mask[..., None], dd, d)
    cv.a[:] = np.argmin(d, -1)


def paint(stop=99, seed=3):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    SKYP = ['paper', 'mist', 'skyp', 'sky', 'pk1', 'pk2']

    if stop <= 8: return cv

    cv.flat_stagger([(76, 77), (80, 75), (86, 74), (94, 75), (102, 77), (112, 79), (122, 81), (127, 82)], I['pk4'], lens=(1, 4), seed=1)

    # ---------------- 10-11 lustrous darks, vivid brights, the blue rim, rock planes -------------
    for pts in ([(0, 211), (16, 202), (30, 193), (43, 187)], [(0, 177), (10, 173), (20, 175)],
                [(57, 153), (65, 149), (76, 147)], [(24, 179), (34, 172), (47, 166)], [(75, 162), (84, 157)],
                [(41, 160), (50, 154)], [(103, 214), (116, 208), (126, 209)]):

        x0 = 5 + k * 5; y0 = 216 - k * 3.1

    shr = np.isin(cv.a, [I['g1'], I['g2'], I['g3']]) & ~upper

    dk = shr & (gaussian_filter(refL, 1) < np.quantile(refL[shr], 0.25))
    cv.scatter_dabs(dk, I['viol'], 25, shapes=('blob', 'h2'), seed=10)
    cv.scatter_dabs(rockm & (cv.a == I['rs3']), I['g6'], 10, shapes=('dot',), seed=14)
    cv.snapshot('10-11 darks, brights, blue rims, plane strokes')
    if stop <= 11: return cv

    for (x, y, col) in [(118, 177, 'g5'), (122, 185, 'g6'), (117, 191, 'g5'), (124, 199, 'orange'),

cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/books/oga && python3 - <<'EOF'

def majority(a, passes=1, protect=None):
    """cluster cleanup: each pixel takes the most common index in its 3x3 if it is an orphan
    (fewer than 2 same-index neighbours). Keeps deliberate marks if protect is given."""
    a = a.copy()
    H, W = a.shape
    for _ in range(passes):
        p = np.pad(a, 1, mode='edge')
        nb = np.stack([p[1 + dy:H + 1 + dy, 1 + dx:W + 1 + dx] for dy in (-1, 0, 1) for dx in (-1, 0, 1) if (dy, dx) != (0, 0)])
        same = (nb == a[None]).sum(0)
        orphan = same < 2
        if protect is not None:
            orphan &= ~protect
        # mode of neighbours
        vals = np.sort(nb, 0)
        best = vals[0].copy(); bestc = np.zeros_like(a); run = np.ones_like(a); 
        for k in range(1, 8):
            eq = vals[k] == vals[k - 1]
            run = np.where(eq, run + 1, 1)
            upd = run > bestc
            best = np.where(upd, vals[k], best); bestc = np.where(upd, run, bestc)
        upd0 = bestc < 1
        a[orphan] = best[orphan]
    return a
'''

"""Master copy 2: C-0007 background (p.60), the path by the stone wall, at 176x99.

Order (Oga's): masses flat first -> each mass split into light and shadow shapes -> the crowd
(clumps of foliage, tufts of grass, stones) as organised members -> points and brights.

Layout ("carbon transfer"): the mass boundaries are my hand polygons read off copies/c0007_ref_grid.png;
where a mass's light/shadow shape follows his, it is his value design smoothed to shapes.
Marks: my brush glyphs (lobed clumps, tuft strokes, stones) placed where his painting has those
members, drawn in my palette.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, maximum_filter, minimum_filter, label
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority

REF = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/copies/c0007_ref_grid_small.png').convert('RGB')
W, H = REF.size
refLab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(REF, float) / 255)
refL = refLab[..., 0]


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(
    # meadow, lit: lemon -> yellow green (the unmuddied ramp, chroma rising into the light)
    m1=c(0.94, 0.16, 106), m2=c(0.87, 0.17, 112), m3=c(0.79, 0.16, 118), m4=c(0.70, 0.14, 124),
    # meadow shadow and tufts
    t1=c(0.58, 0.11, 130), t2=c(0.46, 0.08, 136),
    # forest
    f1=c(0.80, 0.16, 114), f2=c(0.69, 0.15, 122), f3=c(0.57, 0.12, 130), f4=c(0.44, 0.08, 138),
    f5=c(0.31, 0.045, 150), f6=c(0.21, 0.025, 165),
    # stones
    s1=c(0.90, 0.07, 102), s2=c(0.78, 0.05, 108), s3=c(0.60, 0.035, 115), s4=c(0.40, 0.03, 125),
    # path
    p1=c(0.78, 0.11, 78), p2=c(0.64, 0.09, 75), p3=c(0.42, 0.05, 90),
    # the cast shadow band: cool deep green with a sheen
    b1=c(0.25, 0.03, 160), b2=c(0.32, 0.04, 150), b3=c(0.40, 0.055, 140),
    post=c(0.55, 0.06, 70),
)
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def ref_q(mask, names, sigma=0.9, qs=None):
    """split a mask into len(names) value shapes following his smoothed L (quantiles within mask)."""
    Ls = gaussian_filter(refL, sigma)
    qs = qs or list(np.linspace(0, 1, len(names) + 1)[1:-1])
    t = np.quantile(Ls[mask], qs)
    k = np.searchsorted(t, Ls)
    out = np.zeros((H, W), int)
    for j, n in enumerate(names):
        out[mask & (k == j)] = I[n]
    return out


def lobed_clump(cv, x, y, r, lit, mid, dark, seed=0, where=None):
    """one foliage clump as he paints it (C-0007, C-0827): a flat lit shape with a lobed top edge,
    a mid-value belly, a dark pocket under it. The lobes are the leaf-cluster size (~1-2 px here)."""
    rr = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    ang = np.arctan2(yy - y, xx - x)
    lobes = 1 + 0.22 * np.sin(ang * rr.integers(4, 7) + rr.uniform(0, 6)) + 0.1 * rr.standard_normal()
    d = np.hypot((xx - x) / 1.15, (yy - y)) / (r * lobes)
    body = d < 1
    if where is not None:
        body &= where
    # dark pocket below/left (cast into the mass beneath)
    pocket = (np.hypot((xx - x + 0.6 * r) / 1.2, (yy - y - 0.9 * r)) < 0.8 * r) & ~body
    if where is not None:
        pocket &= where
    cv.a[pocket] = dark
    cv.a[body] = mid
    # light from the upper right: the lit shape is the upper-right part of the body
    litp = body & (((xx - x) * 0.55 - (yy - y) * 0.85) > -0.15 * r)
    cv.a[litp] = lit


def paint(stop=99, seed=2):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['m3'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    poly = cv.mask_poly
    # ---------------- masses (flat) --------------------------------------------------------------
    forest = poly([(20, 0), (176, 0), (176, 84), (150, 80), (130, 72), (112, 62), (100, 50), (96, 40), (90, 30),
                   (82, 22), (60, 20), (40, 14), (22, 8)])
    meadowR = poly([(84, 24), (94, 34), (100, 48), (112, 62), (132, 74), (150, 80), (176, 84), (176, 99), (104, 99),
                    (100, 76), (96, 60), (86, 44), (76, 34), (72, 28)])
    band = poly([(58, 26), (66, 30), (72, 34), (80, 40), (88, 48), (94, 58), (98, 66), (102, 80), (104, 99),
                 (54, 99), (56, 84), (60, 74), (64, 66), (66, 56), (66, 44), (62, 34)])
    wall_top = [(6, 16), (18, 20), (32, 24), (44, 26), (54, 30), (60, 36), (64, 44), (65, 54), (63, 62), (58, 68)]
    wall = poly(wall_top + [(52, 72), (48, 70), (58, 60), (60, 52), (58, 42), (52, 34), (40, 30), (28, 28), (14, 24), (4, 20)])
    wall2 = poly([(0, 60), (14, 60), (30, 64), (42, 66), (52, 70), (58, 76), (58, 99), (44, 99), (44, 80), (36, 72), (20, 70), (0, 68)])
    path_lit = poly([(98, 66), (102, 72), (106, 80), (109, 90), (112, 99), (105, 99), (103, 88), (100, 78), (96, 70)])
    path_up = poly([(68, 30), (72, 28), (80, 22), (86, 20), (86, 22), (78, 26), (72, 32), (70, 36)])
    meadowL = ~forest & ~band & ~meadowR & ~wall & ~wall2
    cv.a[meadowL] = I['m2']; cv.a[meadowR] = I['m4']; cv.a[forest] = I['f4']; cv.a[band] = I['b1']
    cv.a[wall | wall2] = I['s3']; cv.a[path_lit | path_up] = I['p1']
    cv.snapshot('1 masses, flat')
    if stop <= 1: return cv

    # ---------------- light and shadow shapes inside each mass --------------------------------------
    cv.a[meadowL] = ref_q(meadowL, ['m4', 'm3', 'm2', 'm1'], 1.4, [0.15, 0.4, 0.75])[meadowL]
    cv.a[meadowR] = ref_q(meadowR, ['t1', 'm4', 'm3', 'm2'], 1.2, [0.2, 0.5, 0.85])[meadowR]
    cv.a[forest] = ref_q(forest, ['f6', 'f5', 'f4', 'f3'], 1.0, [0.25, 0.5, 0.75])[forest]
    cv.a[band] = ref_q(band, ['b1', 'b2', 'b3'], 1.5, [0.6, 0.9])[band]
    pd = band & (gaussian_filter(np.abs(refLab[..., 2] / (np.hypot(refLab[..., 1], refLab[..., 2]) + 1e-6)), 0.5) > 0.9) & (xx > 70)
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
    cv.snapshot('2 light & shadow shapes (his value design, smoothed)')
    if stop <= 2: return cv

    # ---------------- the crowd: foliage clumps where his lit clumps are -----------------------------
    Ls = gaussian_filter(refL, 0.8)
    peaks = (Ls == maximum_filter(Ls, 5)) & forest & (Ls > np.quantile(Ls[forest], 0.45))
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(peaks)
    order = np.argsort(ys)          # paint far (top) first, near (bottom) overlaps
    for k in order:
        x, y = xs[k], ys[k]
        v = Ls[y, x]
        r = 2.2 + 3.0 * (y / H) + (1 if v > np.quantile(Ls[forest], 0.85) else 0)
        lit = I['f1'] if v > np.quantile(Ls[forest], 0.8) else I['f2']
        lobed_clump(cv, x, y, r, lit, I['f3'], I['f5'], seed=int(x * 131 + y), where=forest)
    # clumps in the right meadow's bushes (the lit bush left of the path, lower right bushes)
    cv.snapshot('3 the crowd: lobed clumps where his lit clumps are')
    if stop <= 3: return cv

    # ---------------- grass tufts: short dark strokes where his meadow has dark clusters -------------
    rr = np.random.default_rng(seed + 5)
    for mask, dark, lite in ((meadowL, 't1', 'm1'), (meadowR, 't2', 'm2')):
        lo = (Ls == minimum_filter(Ls, 3)) & mask
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(lo)
        for x, y in zip(xs, ys):
            n = 1 + int(3 * y / H)                  # tufts grow with nearness
            for j in range(n):
                xx0 = x + rr.integers(-1, 2)
                L = 1 + int(rr.integers(1, 3) * (0.5 + y / H))
                lean = rr.choice([-0.5, 0, 0.5])
                cv.blade(xx0, y, I[dark], length=L, lean=lean)
            if rr.uniform() < 0.3:
                cv.put(x + 1, y - L, I[lite])        # a lit blade tip beside the tuft
    cv.snapshot('4 grass tufts (nearer = longer)')
    if stop <= 4: return cv

    # ---------------- stones of the wall: rounded, lit top, dark under ------------------------------
    def stone(x, y, w, h):
        m = (((xx - x) / w) ** 2 + ((yy - y) / h) ** 2) < 1
        cv.a[m] = I['s2']
        cv.a[m & (yy < y - 0.2 * h)] = I['s1']
        cv.a[m & (yy > y + 0.35 * h)] = I['s3']
        under = (((xx - x) / w) ** 2 + ((yy - y - h) / (0.5 * h)) ** 2) < 1
        cv.a[under & ~m] = I['s4']
    # along the upper wall (small, far) then the near cross wall (bigger)
    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(wall_top[1:]):
        stone(x, y + 1, 2.4, 1.6)
    for (x, y) in [(46, 34), (52, 38), (57, 44), (60, 50), (61, 57), (58, 63), (54, 67)]:
        stone(x, y, 2.6, 1.8)
    for (x, y, w, h) in [(4, 62, 4, 2.6), (12, 63, 3.6, 2.4), (20, 65, 3.8, 2.6), (28, 67, 4, 2.6), (36, 69, 4, 2.8),
                         (44, 72, 4.2, 3), (50, 77, 4.4, 3), (50, 84, 4.6, 3.2), (51, 91, 4.8, 3.4), (51, 97, 5, 3.4)]:
        stone(x, y, w, h)
    cv.snapshot('5 stones: lit tops, dark undersides')
    if stop <= 5: return cv

    # ---------------- points: the post, sheen on the band, path edge ---------------------------------
    cv.line([(37, 44), (37, 60)], I['post'])
    cv.line([(71, 36), (76, 42), (82, 48), (88, 56), (93, 64)], I['p3'])   # the path, dim, inside the shadow
    cv.snapshot('6 points')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv = paint()
    import os; os.makedirs(f'{ROOT}/copies/out', exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/copies/out/c0007_copy.png')
    cells = [zoom(cv.pal[a], 2) for _, a in cv.stages]
    grid(cells, 3, [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='Master copy 2: C-0007, stage by stage (176x99, 2x)').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/copy2_c0007_stages.png')
    plate = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/plates/lv/C0007_bg_in.png')
    grid([fit(plate, w=704), zoom(REF, 4), zoom(cv.rgb(), 4)], 1,
         ['his C-0007 (scan)', 'his, area-reduced to 176 px, 4x', 'my copy, 4x'], title='Master copy 2: C-0007').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/copy2_c0007.png')

Write: books/oga/copies/p1_foliage.py
"""Passage copy P1: broadleaf crowns seen from slightly above (C-0007 right side), 72x60 px.

What his crowns are made of (looked at in plates/lv/C0007_bg_in.png at full size):
  - flat lit PLATES: leafy wedges laid by the flat brush, wider than tall, with a serrated top edge
    (1-px teeth) and a lighter lip along that top edge;
  - each plate sits over a DARK POCKET (the shadow cast by the plate into the crown below);
  - plates stack in tiers, lower ones wider; between trees, big dark holes stay unpainted-dark;
  - one bush is stippled: yellow-green dots on dark (a different species, a point).
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, maximum_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(d1=c(0.20, 0.03, 165), d2=c(0.30, 0.05, 150), m1=c(0.42, 0.085, 138), m2=c(0.53, 0.11, 132),
         l1=c(0.63, 0.14, 128), l2=c(0.72, 0.16, 124), l3=c(0.81, 0.17, 118), y1=c(0.78, 0.15, 105),
         mead=c(0.66, 0.14, 125), meadl=c(0.76, 0.15, 118), ochre=c(0.70, 0.10, 85))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def plate(cv, x, y, w, h, body, lip, pocket, seed=0, where=None):
    """a flat lit plate: width w, height h, serrated top, lighter lip, dark pocket below."""
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    H, W = cv.a.shape
    x0, x1 = int(round(x - w / 2)), int(round(x + w / 2))
    # top edge: a shallow arch with 1-px teeth
    for xx in range(x0, x1 + 1):
        if not (0 <= xx < W):
            continue
        t = (xx - x) / (w / 2 + 1e-6)
        top = y - h * 0.5 * (1 - t * t) ** 0.5 + (r.integers(0, 2) if abs(t) < 0.95 else 1)
        bot = y + h * 0.5 * (1 - 0.3 * t * t)
        for yy in range(int(round(top)), int(round(bot)) + 1):
            if 0 <= yy < H and (where is None or where[yy, xx]):
                cv.a[yy, xx] = body
        yl = int(round(top))
        if 0 <= yl < H and (where is None or where[yl, xx]) and r.uniform() < 0.8:
            cv.a[yl, xx] = lip
        # pocket: 1-2 px below the plate, offset left (light from the upper right)
        for k in (1, 2):
            yp, xp = int(round(bot)) + k, xx - 1
            if 0 <= yp < H and 0 <= xp < W and (where is None or where[yp, xp]) and (k == 1 or r.uniform() < 0.5):
                cv.a[yp, xp] = pocket


def paint(ref_path=f'{ROOT}/copies/p1_ref.png', seed=1):
    ref = Image.open(ref_path).convert('RGB'); W, H = ref.size
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255); L = lab[..., 0]
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, seed=seed)
    Ls = gaussian_filter(L, 1.2)
    # 1 the ground: three values, his notan, organised (smoothed) -> dark holes, mid crowns, open meadow
    q = np.quantile(Ls, [0.30, 0.62])
    cv.a[:] = np.where(Ls < q[0], I['d1'], np.where(Ls < q[1], I['m1'], I['m2']))
    hue = np.degrees(np.arctan2(lab[..., 2], lab[..., 1])) % 360
    meadow = gaussian_filter((hue < 122).astype(float), 1.5) > 0.5
    cv.a[meadow & (Ls > q[1])] = I['mead']
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
    cv.snapshot('1 ground: notan, 3 values')
    # 2 the second dark: pockets inside the mid masses where he is darker
    Lf = gaussian_filter(L, 0.6)
    cv.a[(cv.a == I['m1']) & (Lf < np.quantile(L, 0.4))] = I['d2']
    cv.snapshot('2 second dark')
    # 3 plates at his lit maxima, far (top) to near (bottom); size grows downward
    Lp = gaussian_filter(L, 0.8)
    pk = (Lp == maximum_filter(Lp, 5)) & (Lp > np.quantile(Lp, 0.5)) & ~meadow
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(pk)
    for k in np.argsort(ys):
        x, y = xs[k], ys[k]
        v = Lp[y, x]
        w = 5 + 4 * y / H + (2 if v > np.quantile(Lp, 0.85) else 0)
        body = I['l2'] if v > np.quantile(Lp, 0.82) else I['l1']
        lip = I['l3'] if body == I['l2'] else I['l2']
        plate(cv, x, y, w, 2.2 + 1.5 * y / H, body, lip, I['d2'], seed=int(x * 97 + y))
    cv.snapshot('3 lit plates with lips and pockets')
    # 4 the stippled bush: yellow dots where his yellow is
    yel = (hue < 112) & (L > 0.5) & ~meadow & (np.hypot(lab[..., 1], lab[..., 2]) > 0.1)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(yel)
    for x, y in zip(xs, ys):
        cv.put(x, y, I['y1'])
    cv.snapshot('4 points: yellow stipple')
    return cv, ref

cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/books/oga && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('copies/p1_foliage.py').read()
a=s.index("    # 3 the lit sheets"); b=s.index("    # 4 the stippled bush")
s=s[:a]+'''    # 3 the members: each crown found as a basin of his smoothed brightness (watershed), then painted
    #   as he paints a crown: lit sheet on the sun side (upper right) with a serrated edge where it
    #   turns into the crown's own shadow, the shadow body, a dark pocket where it tucks under.
    from skimage.segmentation import watershed
    from skimage.feature import peak_local_max
    Lw = gaussian_filter(L, 1.3)
    fg = (Lw > np.quantile(Lw, 0.35)) & ~meadow
    pk = peak_local_max(Lw, min_distance=4, labels=fg.astype(int))
    mk = np.zeros((H, W), int)
    for i, (y, x) in enumerate(pk):
        mk[y, x] = i + 1
    seg = watershed(-Lw, mk, mask=fg)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    order = sorted(range(1, seg.max() + 1), key=lambda i: yy[seg == i].mean() if (seg == i).any() else 0)
    for i in order:
        m = seg == i
        if m.sum() < 6:
            continue
        cy, cx = yy[m].mean(), xx[m].mean()
        ry = max(yy[m].std(), 1.0); rx = max(xx[m].std(), 1.0)
        u = (xx - cx) / rx; v = (yy - cy) / ry
        f = 0.6 * u - 0.8 * v + 0.35 * r.standard_normal((H, W)) * 0.4    # sun from upper right
        vtop = Lw[m].mean()
        bright = vtop > np.quantile(Lw[fg], 0.6)
        cv.a[m] = I['m1'] if not bright else I['m2']
        litm = m & (f > (-0.1 if bright else 0.5))
        # serrated edge: dither the boundary on alternate columns
        edge = litm & ~np.roll(litm, -1, 0)
        cv.a[litm] = I['l2'] if bright else I['l1']
        cv.a[litm & (f > 1.1)] = I['l3'] if bright else I['l2']
        teeth = np.roll(edge, 1, 0) & m & ((xx + yy // 3) % 2 == 0)
        cv.a[teeth] = I['l2'] if bright else I['l1']
        # pocket: lower-left rim of the member
        pocket = m & (f < -1.2)
        cv.a[pocket] = I['d2']
    cv.snapshot('3 members: each crown lit on its sun side, serrated turn, pocket')
'''+s[b:]
# drop old step 4 edges if present
a=s.find("    # 4 the leafy edge"); b=s.find("    # 4 the stippled bush")

216. (manager scratchpad)/p2.png

Write: books/oga/copies/p2_peak.py
"""Passage copy P2: the far peak in mist, from the cover (steps 5-6), 60x84 px.

What he does (steps 5-6, p.7-8, and the finished cover):
  - the peak is painted INTO the wet sky with the sky's own colours: its greys are the mist greys,
    one step darker for the shading;
  - two layers (the upper peak, the lower conifer ridge) overlap with a tongue of mist between;
    the upper, farther one is paler and bluer (aerial perspective, p.54);
  - the conifers are made by the flat brush shifted vertically: vertical dashes, staggered,
    tips breaking the ridge line upward;
  - the foot of each layer dissolves into mist: no bottom edge, the value lifts to white in tongues.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(paper=c(1.0, 0, 0), mist=c(0.955, 0.01, 225), g0=c(0.90, 0.015, 215), g1=c(0.84, 0.02, 210),
         g2=c(0.78, 0.025, 205), g3=c(0.72, 0.03, 195), g4=c(0.65, 0.035, 185),
         v1=c(0.62, 0.06, 160), v2=c(0.52, 0.07, 155), v3=c(0.44, 0.06, 158), rose=c(0.80, 0.03, 40))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def paint(ref_path=f'{ROOT}/copies/p2_ref.png', seed=4):
    ref = Image.open(ref_path).convert('RGB'); W, H = ref.size
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255); L = lab[..., 0]
    C = np.hypot(lab[..., 1], lab[..., 2]); hue = np.degrees(np.arctan2(lab[..., 2], lab[..., 1])) % 360
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    Ls = gaussian_filter(L, 0.8)
    # 5 sky: white, mist worked in where he is not pure white
    cv.a[Ls < 0.985] = I['mist']
    cv.snapshot('5 sky: white, mist worked in')
    # 6a the two layers as flat masses (his silhouette), mist tongue between (hand line)
    body = Ls < 0.93
    split = (yy - (31 + 0.12 * (xx - 20))) > 0              # the mist tongue line under the upper peak
    upper = body & ~split
    lower = body & split
    cv.a[upper] = I['g1']
    cv.a[lower] = I['g2']
    cv.snapshot('6a two layers, flat: upper paler, lower darker')
    # 6b one step darker for the shading: his value design inside each layer, organised
    for m, a, b in ((upper, 'g1', 'g2'), (lower, 'g2', 'g3')):
        t = np.quantile(Ls[m], 0.45)
        cv.a[m & (Ls < t)] = I[b]
    cv.a[lower & (Ls < np.quantile(Ls[lower], 0.15))] = I['g4']
    # the foot dissolves: toward the mist, lift to g0/mist in ragged tongues (his light areas)
    n = cv.noise(0.9)
    for m in (upper, lower):
        cv.a[m & (Ls + 0.012 * n > 0.885)] = I['g0']
        cv.a[m & (Ls + 0.012 * n > 0.915)] = I['mist']
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 1)
    cv.snapshot('6b one step darker; feet dissolve into mist')
    # 6c conifers: the flat brush shifted vertically. along the lower ridge top: tips break upward;
    #    inside: rows of short vertical dashes one step darker, staggered
    top = np.array([np.argmax(lower[:, x]) if lower[:, x].any() else -1 for x in range(W)])
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for x in range(W):
        if top[x] <= 0:
            continue
        if r.uniform() < 0.65:
            for k in range(r.integers(1, 4)):
                cv.put(x, top[x] - k, I['g3'])
    for row in range(0, H, 3):
        for x in range(W):
            y = row + (x % 2) + r.integers(0, 2)
            if 0 <= y < H and lower[y, x] and (r.uniform() < 0.35):
                L0 = r.integers(1, 3)
                dk = I['g4'] if cv.a[y, x] in (I['g3'], I['g4']) else I['g3']
                for k in range(L0):
                    if y + k < H and lower[y + k, x] and cv.a[y + k, x] not in (I['g0'], I['mist']):
                        cv.a[y + k, x] = dk
    # the upper peak: sparser, paler staggers along its ridge (farther away)
    topu = np.array([np.argmax(upper[:, x]) if upper[:, x].any() else -1 for x in range(W)])
    for x in range(0, W, 2):
        if topu[x] > 0 and r.uniform() < 0.5:
            for k in range(r.integers(1, 3)):
                cv.put(x, topu[x] + k, I['g2'])
    cv.snapshot('6c conifers: flat brush staggered, tips break the ridge')
    # the green patch where his colour is green, with its own staggered dark
    green = gaussian_filter(((hue > 140) & (hue < 175) & (C > 0.03)).astype(float), 0.8) > 0.5
    cv.a[green] = I['v1']
    cv.a[green & (Ls < np.quantile(Ls[green], 0.5))] = I['v2']
    for x in range(W):
        for y in range(H):
            if green[y, x] and (x + y * 3) % 5 == 0 and r.uniform() < 0.6:
                cv.put(x, y, I['v3']); cv.put(x, y + 1, I['v3'])
    # the pink rock at the bottom right (his warm accent)
    rose = gaussian_filter(((hue < 80) & (L > 0.7) & (yy > 60)).astype(float), 1.0) > 0.5
    cv.a[rose] = I['rose']
    cv.snapshot('green patch, warm rock')
    return cv, ref


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid
    cv, ref = paint()
    cells = [zoom(ref, 6)] + [zoom(cv.pal[a], 6) for _, a in cv.stages]
    grid(cells, 4, ['his, reduced'] + [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='P2 far peak in mist (cover), 6x').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p2_peak.png')

    # coloured greys: the mist-wrapped lower left goes lavender (his greys are never one hue)
    zone = (xx < 30) & (yy > 44)
    cv.a[zone & (cv.a == I['g1'])] = I['lav1']
    cv.a[zone & (cv.a == I['g2'])] = I['lav2']""")
open('copies/p2_peak.py','w').write(s)

Write: books/oga/copies/p3_rocks.py
"""Passage copy P3: the near rocks of the cover (steps 7, 10, 11), 72x90 px. Painted entirely by hand.

What he does:
  - a rock is two or three PLANES, decided by lines: the top plane takes the sky light (pale lavender,
    pinkish where warm), the faces are in shadow (warm dark olive-brown, never a lowered grey);
  - the shadow face is uneven paint (塗りムラ): greener and browner patches, a few lichen dots;
  - on the edge where the lit top turns into the shadow face on the far side, a hard line of pure
    cobalt: the sky's colour at the edge of the light (際, p.56) — the cover's signature;
  - brush direction on the lit plane follows the plane (streaks along it, step 11 "raise the texture").
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb
from copykit import Canvas


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(paper=c(1.0, 0, 0), airy=c(0.86, 0.05, 125), airy2=c(0.76, 0.07, 125),
         top1=c(0.86, 0.025, 330), top2=c(0.77, 0.035, 300), top3=c(0.68, 0.03, 290), warm=c(0.80, 0.04, 50),
         rim=c(0.55, 0.17, 268),
         s1=c(0.45, 0.03, 75), s2=c(0.37, 0.028, 85), s3=c(0.30, 0.025, 95), s4=c(0.23, 0.018, 110), sg=c(0.38, 0.04, 140),
         lich=c(0.72, 0.10, 90), gr1=c(0.55, 0.10, 125), gr2=c(0.70, 0.13, 115), gr3=c(0.84, 0.15, 105), crack=c(0.18, 0.015, 120))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])
W, H = 72, 90


def rock(cv, outline, top, rim=None, top_idx='top2', lit_first=True, seed=0):
    m = cv.mass(outline, I['s2'], wet=0.3)
    cv.mottle(m, I['s1'], 0.28, 1.6)
    cv.mottle(m, I['s3'], 0.30, 1.3)
    cv.mottle(m, I['sg'], 0.12, 1.2)
    cv.mottle(m, I['s4'], 0.10, 0.9)
    t = cv.mass(top, I[top_idx], wet=0.4, where=m) if top else None
    if t is not None:
        cv.mottle(t, I['top1'], 0.22, 1.0)
        cv.mottle(t, I['top3'], 0.18, 1.0)
    if rim:
        cv.line(rim, I['rim'])
    return m, t


def paint(seed=5):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    # ground: airy shrubs top left against paper, grass band behind the rocks
    cv.mass([(0, 20), (10, 10), (24, 4), (40, 0), (72, 0), (72, 30), (40, 34), (0, 40)], I['airy'], wet=2.5, wet_scale=0.8)
    cv.mottle(cv.a == I['airy'], I['airy2'], 0.4, 1.0)
    cv.mass([(18, 34), (40, 28), (60, 34), (70, 46), (60, 56), (40, 50)], I['gr1'], wet=1.2)
    cv.snapshot('ground: airy shrubs, grass band')
    # the rocks, back to front
    rock(cv, [(22, 24), (30, 16), (40, 12), (46, 14), (44, 22), (34, 28), (24, 30)],
         [(22, 24), (30, 16), (40, 12), (46, 14), (42, 18), (32, 22), (24, 27)], rim=[(40, 12), (46, 14)], top_idx='top1')
    rock(cv, [(54, 6), (64, 3), (72, 4), (72, 22), (60, 23), (53, 16)],
         [(54, 6), (64, 3), (72, 4), (72, 7), (62, 7), (55, 9)], rim=[(58, 6), (64, 5), (72, 7)])
    rock(cv, [(52, 27), (61, 25), (67, 31), (66, 41), (57, 44), (51, 36)],
         [(52, 27), (61, 25), (66, 30), (58, 31), (53, 31)], rim=[(61, 25), (67, 31)])
    rock(cv, [(0, 28), (8, 25), (14, 26), (20, 30), (23, 35), (22, 45), (16, 53), (8, 58), (0, 62)],
         [(0, 28), (8, 25), (14, 26), (9, 30), (2, 33), (0, 35)], rim=[(14, 26), (18, 29), (22, 34)])
    mB, tB = rock(cv, [(0, 72), (10, 62), (22, 52), (32, 41), (38, 34), (46, 34), (53, 40), (59, 48), (65, 56),
                       (67, 70), (63, 90), (0, 90)],
                  [(0, 72), (10, 62), (22, 52), (32, 41), (38, 34), (42, 35), (36, 44), (26, 55), (15, 66), (5, 76), (0, 80)],
                  rim=[(46, 35), (53, 41), (60, 49)])
    cv.snapshot('rocks: planes by drawn lines, mottled shadow faces, cobalt rims')
    # plane texture: streaks along the top plane of B (brush follows the plane)
    for k in range(10):
        x0 = 2 + k * 3.6; y0 = 74 - k * 3.9
        cv.line([(x0, y0), (x0 + 3, y0 - 3)], I['top1'] if k % 2 else I['warm'])
    # crack between A and B, the dark tuck under the top plane
    cv.line([(0, 64), (8, 60), (16, 55), (22, 47)], I['crack'])
    cv.line([(4, 79), (14, 69), (24, 58), (34, 47), (40, 38)], I['s4'])
    cv.scatter_dabs(mB & ~tB, I['lich'], 6, shapes=('dot', 'h2'), seed=seed)
    cv.snapshot('plane streaks, crack, lichen')
    # grass tufts between the rocks: blades with lemon tips
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for i in range(22):
        x = int(r.uniform(24, 62)); y = int(r.uniform(30, 48))
        cv.blade(x, y, I['gr2'], length=int(r.integers(2, 5)), lean=r.choice([-0.4, 0, 0.4]), tip=I['gr3'])
    cv.snapshot('grass tufts, lemon tips')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid
    cv = paint()
    ref = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/copies/p3_ref.png')
    cells = [zoom(ref, 5)] + [zoom(cv.pal[a], 5) for _, a in cv.stages] + [zoom(ref, 2), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)]
    grid(cells, 5, ['his, reduced'] + [n for n, _ in cv.stages] + ['his 2x', 'mine 2x'], title='P3 near rocks (cover), 5x').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p3_rocks.png')

Write: books/oga/copies/p1_foliage.py
"""Passage copy P1 (v2): broadleaf crowns seen from slightly above (C-0007 right side), 72x60 px.

Critic's read of v1: "sawtooth scales, one cool green family, dead flat darks: the material is
wrong". His crowns are ROUNDED LEAF-CLUMPS with dark holes between them, lit warm (ochre-yellow)
on top, near-black olive in the holes, and the darks still hold dim clumps one value up.

So the vocabulary here is one mark, the clump, drawn at three light levels:
  lit clump : rounded 3-6 px, convex bumps on its upper-right edge in a warm light, body in a warm
              olive-green, a 1-px dark crescent at lower left where it tucks under the next
  mid clump : the same shape one family down (the crown's turning side)
  dim clump : one value above the hole colour, inside the big darks
Placed where his painting has them (his smoothed brightness picks the level), far to near.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(hole=c(0.17, 0.03, 125), dim1=c(0.25, 0.04, 130), dim2=c(0.32, 0.055, 130),
         mid1=c(0.40, 0.075, 128), mid2=c(0.48, 0.095, 124),
         lit1=c(0.57, 0.12, 120), lit2=c(0.66, 0.135, 114), lit3=c(0.76, 0.14, 104), ochre=c(0.80, 0.13, 95),
         mead=c(0.66, 0.13, 118), meadl=c(0.75, 0.14, 110), path=c(0.72, 0.10, 80))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])

LEVELS = {  # (cap, body, crescent)
    'lit': ('lit3', 'lit1', 'mid1'), 'lit+': ('ochre', 'lit2', 'mid1'),
    'mid': ('lit1', 'mid2', 'dim2'), 'dim': ('mid1', 'dim2', 'hole'),
}


def clump(cv, x, y, r, level, seed=0):
    """a rounded leaf-clump. r in px (1.5-3). Light from the upper right."""
    cap, body, cres = (I[k] for k in LEVELS[level])
    rr = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    R = int(np.ceil(r)) + 2
    tilt = rr.uniform(-0.25, 0.25)
    for dy in range(-R, R + 1):
        for dx in range(-R, R + 1):
            # slightly wide ellipse, random tilt, lumpy outline (2-3 px bumps)
            u, v = dx * np.cos(tilt) + dy * np.sin(tilt), -dx * np.sin(tilt) + dy * np.cos(tilt)
            ang = np.arctan2(v, u)
            rad = r * (1 + 0.18 * np.cos(3 * ang + seed) + 0.1 * np.cos(5 * ang + 2 * seed))
            d = np.hypot(u / 1.2, v) / rad
            if d > 1:
                continue
            X, Y = x + dx, y + dy
            s = (dx * 0.6 - dy * 0.8) / (r + 1e-6)          # toward the sun
            idx = body
            if s > 0.35 and d > 0.35:
                idx = cap
            elif s < -0.45 and d > 0.6:
                idx = cres
            cv.put(X, Y, idx)


def paint(ref_path=f'{ROOT}/copies/p1_ref.png', seed=1):
    ref = Image.open(ref_path).convert('RGB'); W, H = ref.size
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255); L = lab[..., 0]
    hue = np.degrees(np.arctan2(lab[..., 2], lab[..., 1])) % 360
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['hole'], seed=seed)
    Ls = gaussian_filter(L, 1.0)
    # ground: holes and crown masses (his notan, about half dark), open meadow
    q = np.quantile(Ls, [0.45, 0.72])
    cv.a[:] = np.where(Ls < q[0], I['hole'], np.where(Ls < q[1], I['dim2'], I['mid2']))
    meadow = gaussian_filter(((hue < 122) & (L > 0.55)).astype(float), 1.5) > 0.55
    cv.a[meadow] = I['mead']
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
    cv.snapshot('1 ground: holes (half), crown masses, meadow')
    # the crowd: clumps on a jittered grid, level from his brightness; far (top) to near (bottom)
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pts = []
    step = 3
    for y in range(0, H + 2, step):
        for x in range(-1, W + 2, step):
            xx = x + r.uniform(-1.2, 1.2) + (step / 2 if (y // step) % 2 else 0)
            yy_ = y + r.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
            pts.append((xx, yy_))
    pts.sort(key=lambda p: p[1])
    qd, qm, ql, qp = np.quantile(Ls[~meadow], [0.30, 0.52, 0.72, 0.9])
    for (x, y) in pts:
        xi, yi = int(np.clip(round(x), 0, W - 1)), int(np.clip(round(y), 0, H - 1))
        if meadow[yi, xi]:
            continue
        v = Ls[yi, xi]
        if v < qd:
            if r.uniform() < 0.35:
                clump(cv, x, y, 1.6, 'dim', seed=int(x * 13 + y * 7))
            continue
        level = 'dim' if v < qm else ('mid' if v < ql else ('lit' if v < qp else 'lit+'))
        rad = 1.6 + 1.2 * (y / H) + (0.5 if level.startswith('lit') else 0)
        clump(cv, x, y, rad, level, seed=int(x * 13 + y * 7))
    cv.snapshot('2 the crowd: rounded clumps at four light levels')
    # points: sunflecks as 1-2 px dots on lit clump edges only (where his yellow is)
    yel = (hue < 112) & (L > 0.5) & ~meadow & (np.hypot(lab[..., 1], lab[..., 2]) > 0.1)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(yel)
    for x, y in zip(xs, ys):
        if cv.a[y, x] in (I['lit1'], I['lit2'], I['lit3']):
            cv.put(x, y, I['ochre'])
    cv.snapshot('3 points: sunflecks on lit edges')
    return cv, ref


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid
    cv, ref = paint()
    cells = [zoom(ref, 8)] + [zoom(cv.pal[a], 8) for _, a in cv.stages]
    grid(cells, 2, ['his, reduced'] + [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='P1 v2 broadleaf crowns (C-0007), 8x').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p1_foliage.png')
    grid([zoom(ref, 2), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2), zoom(ref, 1), zoom(cv.rgb(), 1)], 4, ['his 2x', 'mine 2x', '1x', '1x'], title='P1 at viewing size').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p1_foliage_2x.png')

"""Passage copy P1: broadleaf crowns seen from slightly above (C-0007 right side), 72x60 px.

"""Passage copy P1 (v2): broadleaf crowns seen from slightly above (C-0007 right side), 72x60 px.
(v1, with watershed members and sawtooth edges, is kept in p1_foliage_v1.py.)

Critic's read of v1: "sawtooth scales, one cool green family, dead flat darks: the material is
wrong". His crowns are ROUNDED LEAF-CLUMPS with dark holes between them, lit warm (ochre-yellow)
on top, near-black olive in the holes, and the darks still hold dim clumps one value up.

So the vocabulary here is one mark, the clump, drawn at four light levels:
  lit clump : rounded 3-6 px, convex bumps on its upper-right edge in a warm light, body in a warm
              olive-green, a 1-px dark crescent at lower left where it tucks under the next
  mid clump : the same shape one family down (the crown's turning side)
  dim clump : one value above the hole colour, inside the big darks
Placed where his painting has them (his smoothed brightness picks the level), far to near.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(hole=c(0.17, 0.03, 125), dim1=c(0.25, 0.04, 130), dim2=c(0.32, 0.055, 130),
         mid1=c(0.40, 0.075, 128), mid2=c(0.48, 0.095, 124),
         lit1=c(0.57, 0.12, 120), lit2=c(0.66, 0.135, 114), lit3=c(0.76, 0.14, 104), ochre=c(0.80, 0.13, 95),
         mead=c(0.66, 0.13, 118), meadl=c(0.75, 0.14, 110), path=c(0.72, 0.10, 80))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])

LEVELS = {  # (cap, body, crescent)
    'lit': ('lit3', 'lit1', 'mid1'), 'lit+': ('ochre', 'lit2', 'mid1'),
    'mid': ('lit1', 'mid2', 'dim2'), 'dim': ('mid1', 'dim2', 'hole'),
}


def clump(cv, x, y, r, level, seed=0, pal_levels=None):
    """a rounded leaf-clump. r in px (1.5-3). Light from the upper right."""
    lv = (pal_levels or LEVELS)[level]
    cap, body, cres = (cv_index(cv, k) for k in lv)
    rr = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    R = int(np.ceil(r)) + 2
    tilt = rr.uniform(-0.25, 0.25)
    for dy in range(-R, R + 1):
        for dx in range(-R, R + 1):
            u, v = dx * np.cos(tilt) + dy * np.sin(tilt), -dx * np.sin(tilt) + dy * np.cos(tilt)
            ang = np.arctan2(v, u)
            rad = r * (1 + 0.18 * np.cos(3 * ang + seed) + 0.1 * np.cos(5 * ang + 2 * seed))
            d = np.hypot(u / 1.2, v) / rad
            if d > 1:
                continue
            s = (dx * 0.6 - dy * 0.8) / (r + 1e-6)
            idx = body
            if s > 0.35 and d > 0.35:
                idx = cap
            elif s < -0.45 and d > 0.6:
                idx = cres
            cv.put(x + dx, y + dy, idx)


def cv_index(cv, k):
    return cv.names[k] if hasattr(cv, 'names') else I[k]


def paint(ref_path=f'{ROOT}/copies/p1_ref.png', seed=1):
    ref = Image.open(ref_path).convert('RGB'); W, H = ref.size
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255); L = lab[..., 0]
    hue = np.degrees(np.arctan2(lab[..., 2], lab[..., 1])) % 360
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['hole'], seed=seed)
    Ls = gaussian_filter(L, 1.0)
    q = np.quantile(Ls, [0.45, 0.72])
    cv.a[:] = np.where(Ls < q[0], I['hole'], np.where(Ls < q[1], I['dim2'], I['mid2']))
    meadow = gaussian_filter(((hue < 122) & (L > 0.55)).astype(float), 1.5) > 0.55
    cv.a[meadow] = I['mead']
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
    cv.snapshot('1 ground: holes (half), crown masses, meadow')
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pts = []
    step = 3
    for y in range(0, H + 2, step):
        for x in range(-1, W + 2, step):
            pts.append((x + r.uniform(-1.2, 1.2) + (step / 2 if (y // step) % 2 else 0), y + r.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)))
    pts.sort(key=lambda p: p[1])
    qd, qm, ql, qp = np.quantile(Ls[~meadow], [0.30, 0.52, 0.72, 0.9])
    for (x, y) in pts:
        xi, yi = int(np.clip(round(x), 0, W - 1)), int(np.clip(round(y), 0, H - 1))
        if meadow[yi, xi]:
            continue
        v = Ls[yi, xi]
        if v < qd:
            if r.uniform() < 0.35:
                clump(cv, x, y, 1.6, 'dim', seed=int(x * 13 + y * 7))
            continue
        level = 'dim' if v < qm else ('mid' if v < ql else ('lit' if v < qp else 'lit+'))
        rad = 1.6 + 1.2 * (y / H) + (0.5 if level.startswith('lit') else 0)
        clump(cv, x, y, rad, level, seed=int(x * 13 + y * 7))
    cv.snapshot('2 the crowd: rounded clumps at four light levels')
    yel = (hue < 112) & (L > 0.5) & ~meadow & (np.hypot(lab[..., 1], lab[..., 2]) > 0.1)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(yel)
    for x, y in zip(xs, ys):
        if cv.a[y, x] in (I['lit1'], I['lit2'], I['lit3']):
            cv.put(x, y, I['ochre'])
    cv.snapshot('3 points: sunflecks on lit edges')
    return cv, ref


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid
    cv, ref = paint()
    cells = [zoom(ref, 8)] + [zoom(cv.pal[a], 8) for _, a in cv.stages]
    grid(cells, 2, ['his, reduced'] + [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='P1 v2 broadleaf crowns (C-0007), 8x').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p1_foliage.png')

Write: books/oga/copies/p3_rocks.py
"""Passage copy P3 (v2): the near rocks of the cover (steps 7, 10, 11), 72x90 px. Painted by hand.

What he does:
  - a rock is two or three PLANES, decided by lines: the top plane takes the sky light (pale lavender-
    pink, warmer and lighter at its upper edge, darker toward its lower end), the faces are in shadow
    (warm dark olive-brown, never a lowered grey);
  - faces turned up take cool green-grey reflected light (p.56: reflected light takes the sky's
    colour, loosened with the object's own colour); faces turned down stay the darkest;
  - on the edge where the lit top turns into the shadow face on the far side, a hard line of pure
    cobalt: the sky's colour at the edge of the light (際) — the cover's signature;
  - brush direction on the lit plane follows the plane.
v1 -> v2 after a cold critique: clean 1:1 slab edges, warm slab ramp, facets instead of camo blobs,
continuous crack, olive shrub masses with sky holes, yellow in two clusters.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb
from copykit import Canvas


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(paper=c(1.0, 0, 0), ol1=c(0.50, 0.07, 122), ol2=c(0.62, 0.09, 118), ol3=c(0.74, 0.10, 112), ol0=c(0.40, 0.05, 125),
         top1=c(0.87, 0.035, 25), top2=c(0.77, 0.035, 330), top3=c(0.67, 0.035, 305), warm=c(0.82, 0.05, 55),
         rim=c(0.55, 0.17, 268),
         s1=c(0.44, 0.035, 70), s2=c(0.36, 0.03, 80), s3=c(0.29, 0.025, 95), s4=c(0.22, 0.02, 110),
         refl=c(0.44, 0.035, 170), refl2=c(0.50, 0.03, 190),
         lich=c(0.74, 0.11, 92), gr1=c(0.52, 0.10, 125), gr2=c(0.68, 0.13, 115), yel=c(0.84, 0.15, 102), crack=c(0.16, 0.015, 120))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])
W, H = 72, 90


def facets(cv, outline, faces, base='s2', seed=0):
    """a rock: outline filled with the base shadow colour, then facet polygons, each one flat."""
    m = cv.mass(outline, I[base], wet=0.0)
    for poly, idx in faces:
        cv.mass(poly, I[idx], wet=0.0, where=m)
    return m


def paint(seed=5):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    # ---- ground: olive shrub masses with sky holes against the paper; dark earth; grass band
    for poly in ([(0, 30), (4, 18), (12, 12), (20, 14), (22, 26), (12, 34)], [(18, 12), (28, 4), (38, 6), (40, 16), (30, 22), (22, 20)],
                 [(36, 4), (46, 0), (56, 2), (58, 10), (48, 14), (40, 12)], [(52, 0), (72, 0), (72, 10), (60, 12)]):
        m = cv.mass(poly, I['ol1'], wet=1.5, wet_scale=0.7)
        cv.mottle(m, I['ol2'], 0.35, 1.2)
        cv.mottle(m, I['ol0'], 0.2, 1.0)
    for (x, y, w, h) in [(8, 22, 3, 2), (26, 10, 2, 3), (44, 6, 3, 2), (16, 18, 2, 2)]:   # sky holes
        cv.a[y:y + h, x:x + w] = I['paper']
    cv.mass([(0, 44), (18, 34), (40, 28), (60, 30), (72, 34), (72, 90), (0, 90)], I['s3'], wet=1.0)
    cv.mass([(18, 34), (40, 27), (60, 33), (70, 46), (60, 56), (40, 50)], I['gr1'], wet=1.2)
    cv.snapshot('1 ground: olive shrubs with sky holes, earth, grass band')

    # ---- rocks, back to front: each an outline plus flat facets
    facets(cv, [(22, 24), (30, 16), (40, 12), (46, 14), (44, 22), (34, 28), (24, 30)],
           [([(22, 24), (30, 16), (40, 12), (46, 14), (42, 18), (32, 22), (24, 27)], 'top1'),
            ([(24, 27), (32, 22), (42, 18), (44, 22), (34, 28), (24, 30)], 's1')])
    cv.line([(40, 12), (46, 14)], I['rim'])
    facets(cv, [(54, 6), (64, 3), (72, 4), (72, 22), (60, 23), (53, 16)],
           [([(54, 6), (64, 3), (72, 4), (72, 7), (62, 7), (55, 9)], 'top2'), ([(60, 12), (72, 12), (72, 22), (60, 23)], 's3')])
    cv.line([(58, 6), (64, 5), (72, 7)], I['rim'])
    facets(cv, [(52, 27), (61, 25), (67, 31), (66, 41), (57, 44), (51, 36)],
           [([(52, 27), (61, 25), (66, 30), (58, 31), (53, 31)], 'top2'), ([(53, 31), (58, 31), (60, 42), (57, 44), (51, 36)], 'refl')])
    cv.line([(61, 25), (67, 31)], I['rim'])
    # rock A (left): lit top sliver, a face turned up (reflected), a face turned down (dark)
    facets(cv, [(0, 28), (8, 25), (14, 26), (20, 30), (23, 35), (22, 45), (16, 53), (8, 58), (0, 62)],
           [([(0, 28), (8, 25), (14, 26), (9, 30), (2, 33), (0, 35)], 'top2'),
            ([(0, 35), (2, 33), (9, 30), (14, 26), (20, 31), (12, 40), (0, 44)], 's1'),
            ([(0, 52), (12, 46), (22, 44), (16, 53), (8, 58), (0, 62)], 's4')])
    cv.line([(14, 26), (18, 29), (22, 34)], I['rim'])
    # rock B: the long slab, edges on clean 1:1 steps
    mB = facets(cv, [(0, 64), (10, 60), (22, 50), (36, 36), (38, 34), (46, 34), (53, 40), (59, 48), (65, 56), (67, 70), (63, 90), (0, 90)],
                [([(0, 70), (36, 34), (40, 34), (46, 36), (0, 82)], 'top2'),                 # the top plane
                 ([(46, 36), (53, 41), (60, 49), (65, 56), (66, 66), (46, 70), (30, 62), (40, 44)], 'refl'),  # face turned up
                 ([(30, 62), (46, 70), (66, 66), (67, 70), (63, 90), (20, 90), (12, 80)], 's3'),   # lower face
                 ([(20, 90), (0, 90), (0, 86), (12, 80)], 's4')])
    cv.snapshot('2 rocks: flat facets decided by lines')

    # ---- 10-11: the slab's ramp (light upper edge, darker lower end), rims, crack, plane streaks
    slab = cv.mask_poly([(0, 70), (36, 34), (40, 34), (46, 36), (0, 82)])
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    dist_top = (xx + yy - 70) / np.sqrt(2)             # distance from the 1:1 top edge
    cv.a[slab & (dist_top < 1.6)] = I['top1']
    cv.a[slab & (xx + yy > 82) & (xx < 16)] = I['top3']   # the lower-left end darkens
    for k in range(7):
        x0 = 6 + k * 5
        cv.line([(x0, 70 - x0 + 5), (x0 + 3, 70 - x0 + 2)], I['warm'] if k % 2 else I['top1'])
    cv.line([(46, 35), (53, 41), (60, 49), (65, 56)], I['rim'])
    cv.line([(0, 83), (12, 71), (24, 59), (34, 49), (40, 43), (46, 37)], I['s4'])     # the tuck under the slab
    cv.line([(0, 63), (8, 59), (15, 55), (20, 50), (23, 46)], I['crack'])              # crack between A and B
    cv.line([(40, 76), (52, 74), (60, 78)], I['s4'])                                    # one break in the lower face
    for (x, y) in [(36, 80), (48, 84), (28, 72)]:
        cv.dab(x, y, I['lich'], 'dot')
    cv.snapshot('3 slab ramp, rims, tuck line, crack, lichen')

    # ---- 12: grass tufts, and the yellow in two clusters at right-middle
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for i in range(14):
        x = int(r.uniform(26, 58)); y = int(r.uniform(32, 48))
        cv.blade(x, y, I['gr2'], length=int(r.integers(2, 5)), lean=r.choice([-0.4, 0, 0.4]))
    for (cx, cy) in [(46, 22), (50, 38)]:
        for k in range(6):
            cv.dab(cx + r.integers(-2, 3), cy + r.integers(-2, 2), I['yel'], r.choice(['dot', 'h2', 'v2']))
    cv.snapshot('4 grass tufts, yellow clusters')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':

Bash: cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/books/oga && cat >> LESSONS.md <<'EOF' ## 3. A wrong turn, and the correction After §2 I started building a procedural test world (a heightfield valley
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/books/oga && cat >> LESSONS.md <<'EOF'

## 3. A wrong turn, and the correction

After §2 I started building a procedural test world (a heightfield valley with forest, a voxel-space renderer, cloud shadows, `src/scene.py`, `src/oga.py`) so I could "apply Oga's passes to simulated fields". The coordinator stopped me with a sibling study's lesson: the Genge student read its book, built a renderer, and got generic slabs. The book is not a rule set to feed a renderer; it teaches how to *paint*. Learn to paint the way it teaches first, by copying his paintings in pixels, and check that the copies carry what his carry.

The test world's first render already showed the problem. Oga's passes applied to its fields gave a picture with his colour and none of his construction. I've kept `scene.py` and `oga.py` as an honest record, and `sheets/ex4_gorge.png` (the boulders with the light let in through a gap) as the one piece of it that taught something (§5). From here on the work is copies.

## 4. Master copies

### 4a. Whole-picture copies were too big a bite

- **Cover, first try** (`copies/cover.py`, `sheets/copy1_cover*.png`). I drew the masses as hand polygons read off a gridded reduction and used his own paint list, working in his order (sky, peak, whole colour, ground, finish). It came out worse than a plain area-reduction of his painting: paper-white gaps where my masses didn't meet, blocks where he has gradations, marks in the wrong places.
- **Cover, second try.** I laid the ground from his plate, each pixel taking the nearest of my paints, then added marks. That is only a worse quantiser: muddy, blotchy, orphan pixels everywhere. One good thing came out of it. His cobalt rims, found by hue in his plate and redrawn as 1-px runs, sit exactly where he put them.
- **C-0007** (`copies/c0007.py`, `sheets/copy2_c0007.png`). Masses by hand, the path and dark band taken from his value and hue classes, then clumps, tufts and stones. The composition carries. The materials don't: one stone shape stamped 25 times, dark orphan pixels over the field, a lime cast, forest crowns as blocks.

Like the master-copy student, I went down to **passages**: one small crop of one material, his reduced crop beside mine at 5–8×, iterated until it holds, then looked at 1–2×.

### 4b. P1: broadleaf crowns from above (C-0007) — `copies/p1_foliage.py`, `sheets/p1_foliage*.png`

- **v1** (`copies/p1_foliage_v1.py`):
  - A three-value notan ground from his brightness, smoothed. On its own this is already very Oga: masses readable "by silhouette alone" (p.47).
  - Then flat plates with serrated tops, stamped at his bright points. They read as rows of mushrooms.
  - Then leafy edges: teeth hanging below lit sheets and lips above. I lipped every top edge, so everything got a halo.
  - Then watershed "members", each crown lit on its sun side. At 1× that read as trees for the first time. At 8× the crowns were Voronoi polygons with sawtooth edges.
- **Cold critic on v1:** "sawtooth scales, like feathers or fern fronds … one cool green family, no warm end and no deep end … the dark block is flat, dead fill; his dark still holds dim clumps." And across all four sheets: "the student is still building each picture from tone patches and texture fill. What his painting carries is **shape vocabulary per material**." That is the book's own point: "for a crowd you must know beforehand what shape each brush motion makes" (p.50).
- **v2: one mark, the rounded leaf-clump**, at four light levels:
  - lit: a warm cap on the upper-right bumps, a warm olive body, and a 1-px dark crescent at the lower left where it tucks under;
  - mid;
  - dim: one value above the hole colour, so the darks keep life;
  - lit+: rare.

  Clumps sit on a jittered grid and are painted far to near. His smoothed brightness picks each clump's level, and about half the area is hole. My first palette went ochre-yellow, overdoing the critic's "warm end"; his lights are fresh mid-green with yellow only at the hot spots. At 2× and 1× it now reads as a broadleaf canopy with holes and a dark mass, close to his.
- **Rule:** a broadleaf crown is clumps, not a texture. Lit caps face the sun, the pocket tucks lower-left, darks hold dim clumps, and about half the area is hole.

### 4c. P2: far peak in mist (cover, steps 5–6) — `copies/p2_peak.py`, `sheets/p2_peak.png`

- **Carries:** two layers, the far one paler; one step darker for the shading; the feet dissolving into mist; flat-brush staggers for conifers, with tips breaking the ridge line.
- **The critic's corrections:**
  - the upper peak should be a mauve-grey, not mint;
  - the mist must cut *across* the mountain in bands, not just frame it;
  - the crag needs fall-line striation;
  - conifer ticks must come in irregular groups, not a comb;
  - no hard-edged lavender slab;
  - the warm rock is flat planes, not stipple.
- **v2 does all of that.** My first mauve was far too saturated; the right colour is a low-chroma blue-violet grey (C ≈ 0.015). His greys are the mist's greys. The striation still reads a little like rain.
- **Rule:** the far peak is painted with the sky's colours, one step darker. Mist comes in lobes across the mass. Conifers are staggered vertical dashes in irregular groups, and the crag gets fall-line strokes.

### 4d. P3: near rocks (cover, steps 7, 10, 11) — `copies/p3_rocks.py`, `sheets/p3_rocks.png`

- **v1** carried at 2×: the lit slab and the cobalt rim. But the slab edge was a jagged staircase, the shadow faces were three-brown camouflage, and the upper boulder floated on the sky.
- **v2** is painted entirely by hand:
  - each rock is an outline plus flat facets drawn as polygons;
  - the slab's edges run on clean 1:1 steps;
  - the slab ramp is warm pink at the upper edge, lavender in the body, darker at its lower end;
  - a green-grey reflected strip sits on the face turned up to the sky (p.56);
  - the lower faces are darkest;
  - a continuous crack and a tuck line run under the slab;
  - olive shrubs with 2–3 px sky holes, and the yellow in two clusters.

  Pure flat facets looked like vector art. Oga's uneven paint (塗りムラ) is part of the material, so every facet gets broad patches of its *adjacent* step only.
- **Rule:** decide a rock's planes with lines. The top plane takes the sky light, faces turned up take cool reflected light, faces turned down stay darkest, and the far edge of the lit plane gets a line of the sky's pure colour. Unevenness stays inside a facet and within one step.
EOF
