Write: books/oga/src/vocab.py
"""The shape vocabulary, one mark per material, as tested in the passage copies.

Each mark is drawn onto a copykit.Canvas and takes palette indices by name through a dict `ix`.
Nothing here decides where to paint; a scene script places the marks (by hand, or where a plan says).

  sky_bands      celeste ramp, flat bands with ragged 1-px seams, paler toward the horizon (C-0772)
  mist_lobe      a lobe of mist cutting across a mass, broken edge (cover, P2)
  stagger        the flat brush shifted vertically: conifers on a far ridge (cover step 6, P2)
  spire          one conifer at middle distance: 1-px leader, tiers widening down, lit ticks on the sun side
  conifer_mass   a stand of spires, far to near, tips breaking its top edge, dark holes between
  clump          a broadleaf leaf-clump at a light level (P1)
  clump_mass     a broadleaf crown mass as clumps on a jittered grid, level from a light field (P1)
  facet_rock     a rock/wall as flat facets decided by lines, uneven paint inside each facet (P3)
  fall_strokes   1-px dark strokes down the fall line of a rock face (P2)
  rim            a 1-px line of the sky's colour on the far edge of a lit plane (P3, 際)
  tufts          grass strokes, longer when nearer, lit tips (C-0007, C-0095)
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, distance_transform_edt


def _rng(seed):
    return np.random.default_rng(abs(int(seed)) % (2 ** 32))


def sky_bands(cv, mask, names, ix, y0, y1, seam=0.8, seed=0):
    """bands from names[0] at y0 (zenith side) to names[-1] at y1 (horizon)."""
    H, W = cv.a.shape
    yy = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W][0]
    t = np.clip((yy - y0) / max(1, (y1 - y0)), 0, 0.9999) * len(names)
    n = cv.noise(0.8, seed)
    # flat bands: the noise only acts near a seam
    f = t - np.floor(t)
    near = (f < 0.2) | (f > 0.8)
    q = np.floor(t + np.where(near, seam * 0.25 * n, 0)).astype(int)
    q = np.clip(q, 0, len(names) - 1)
    cv.a[mask] = np.array([ix[k] for k in names])[q][mask]


def mist_lobe(cv, poly, inner, outer, ix, wet=1.4):
    lobe = cv.mass(poly, ix[outer], wet=wet)
    er = distance_transform_edt(lobe)
    cv.a[lobe & (er + 0.8 * cv.noise(0.8) > 1.8)] = ix[inner]
    return lobe


def stagger(cv, ridge, name, ix, lens=(1, 4), group=(2, 6), gap=(1, 4), up=True, seed=0):
    """vertical dashes along a ridge polyline, in irregular groups. up=True: tips rise above the line."""
    r = _rng(seed)
    rx = np.array([p[0] for p in ridge], float); ry = np.array([p[1] for p in ridge], float)
    x = int(rx.min())
    while x <= rx.max():
        g = int(r.integers(*group))
        for xx in range(x, min(int(rx.max()) + 1, x + g)):
            y = np.interp(xx, rx, ry)
            L = int(r.integers(lens[0], lens[1] + 1))
            for k in range(L):
                cv.put(xx, y - k if up else y + k, ix[name])
        x += g + int(r.integers(*gap))


def spire(cv, x, yb, h, ix, body='c_mid', dark='c_dark', lit='c_lit', sun=+1, w=None, seed=0, where=None):
    """one conifer: base at (x, yb), height h px. Tiers: every 2-3 rows a slightly wider row; the sun side
    (sun=+1 right, -1 left) gets lit tick pixels at tier ends; the shade side a dark edge."""
    r = _rng(seed)
    w = w if w is not None else max(1.0, h * 0.22)
    top = yb - h
    for y in range(int(top), int(yb) + 1):
        t = (y - top) / max(h, 1)
        half = w * t
        tier = ((y - int(top)) % 3 == 2)
        if tier:
            half += 0.8
        x0, x1 = int(round(x - half)), int(round(x + half))
        for xx in range(x0, x1 + 1):
            if where is not None and not (0 <= y < cv.H and 0 <= xx < cv.W and where[y, xx]):
                continue
            side = (xx - x) * sun
            idx = ix[body]
            if side < -half * 0.3:
                idx = ix[dark]
            if tier and side >= half - 1.0 and half > 0.8:
                idx = ix[lit]
            cv.put(xx, y, idx)
    cv.put(x, top, ix[dark] if sun else ix[body])


def conifer_mass(cv, mask, ix, h_range=(6, 14), spacing=3.5, sun=+1, levels=None, light=None, seed=0,
                 fill='c_hole'):
    """a stand: fill the mask dark, then spires on a jittered grid, far (top) to near (bottom).
    light: optional field 0..1; lit trees use levels['lit'], shaded ones levels['shade'] name triples."""
    r = _rng(seed)
    cv.a[mask] = ix[fill]
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return
    levels = levels or {'lit': ('c_mid', 'c_dark', 'c_lit'), 'shade': ('c_dark', 'c_hole', 'c_mid')}
    pts = []
    for y in np.arange(ys.min(), ys.max() + h_range[1], spacing * 0.8):
        for x in np.arange(xs.min() - 2, xs.max() + 3, spacing):
            pts.append((x + r.uniform(-1.2, 1.2), y + r.uniform(-1, 1)))
    pts.sort(key=lambda p: p[1])
    for (x, y) in pts:
        xi, yi = int(round(x)), int(round(y))
        if not (0 <= xi < cv.W and 0 <= yi < cv.H) or not mask[yi, xi]:
            continue
        t = (yi - ys.min()) / max(1, ys.max() - ys.min())
        h = h_range[0] + (h_range[1] - h_range[0]) * t * r.uniform(0.7, 1.1)
        lv = 'lit' if (light is None or light[yi, xi] > 0.5) else 'shade'
        b, d, l = levels[lv]
        spire(cv, x, y + h * 0.35, h, ix, body=b, dark=d, lit=l, sun=sun, seed=int(x * 31 + y * 17))


def clump(cv, x, y, r, cap, body, cres, ix, seed=0, where=None):
    rr = _rng(seed)
    R = int(np.ceil(r)) + 2
    tilt = rr.uniform(-0.25, 0.25)
    for dy in range(-R, R + 1):
        for dx in range(-R, R + 1):
            u, v = dx * np.cos(tilt) + dy * np.sin(tilt), -dx * np.sin(tilt) + dy * np.cos(tilt)
            ang = np.arctan2(v, u)
            rad = r * (1 + 0.18 * np.cos(3 * ang + seed) + 0.1 * np.cos(5 * ang + 2 * seed))
            if np.hypot(u / 1.2, v) / rad > 1:
                continue
            X, Y = int(round(x + dx)), int(round(y + dy))
            if where is not None and not (0 <= Y < cv.H and 0 <= X < cv.W and where[Y, X]):
                continue
            d = np.hypot(u / 1.2, v) / rad
            s = (dx * 0.6 - dy * 0.8) / (r + 1e-6)
            idx = ix[body]
            if s > 0.5 and d > 0.45:
                idx = ix[cap]
            elif s < -0.45 and d > 0.6:
                idx = ix[cres]
            cv.put(X, Y, idx)


def clump_mass(cv, mask, ix, light, levels, r_range=(2.0, 3.4), step=4, hole='b_hole', dim_share=0.35, seed=0):
    """levels: list of (threshold, (cap, body, cres)) ascending; light field 0..1 picks the level."""
    rr = _rng(seed)
    cv.a[mask] = ix[hole]
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return
    pts = []
    for y in range(ys.min() - 1, ys.max() + 3, step):
        for x in range(xs.min() - 2, xs.max() + 3, step):
            pts.append((x + rr.uniform(-1.2, 1.2) + (step / 2 if (y // step) % 2 else 0), y + rr.uniform(-1, 1)))
    pts.sort(key=lambda p: p[1])
    for (x, y) in pts:
        xi, yi = int(np.clip(round(x), 0, cv.W - 1)), int(np.clip(round(y), 0, cv.H - 1))
        if not mask[yi, xi]:
            continue
        v = light[yi, xi]
        lev = None
        for thr, trip in levels:
            if v >= thr:
                lev = trip
        if lev is None:
            if rr.uniform() < dim_share:
                lev = levels[0][1]
            else:
                continue
        t = (yi - ys.min()) / max(1, ys.max() - ys.min())
        clump(cv, x, y, r_range[0] + (r_range[1] - r_range[0]) * t, *lev, ix, seed=int(x * 13 + y * 7), where=mask)


def facet_rock(cv, outline, faces, ix, base, nxt, share=0.28, scale=2.6):
    """outline filled with base; faces [(poly, name)]; nxt: name -> adjacent step for uneven paint."""
    m = cv.mass(outline, ix[base], wet=0.0)
    for poly, name in faces:
        f = cv.mass(poly, ix[name], wet=0.0, where=m)
        if name in nxt:
            cv.mottle(f, ix[nxt[name]], share, scale)
    if base in nxt:
        cv.mottle(m & (cv.a == ix[base]), ix[nxt[base]], share, scale)
    return m


def fall_strokes(cv, mask, name, ix, n=30, lens=(2, 6), drift=0.2, seed=0):
    r = _rng(seed)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=min(n, len(ys)), replace=False):
        x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
        L = int(r.integers(*lens))
        for k in range(L):
            X, Y = int(round(x + drift * k)), y + k
            if 0 <= Y < cv.H and 0 <= X < cv.W and mask[Y, X]:
                cv.a[Y, X] = ix[name]


def rim(cv, pts, name, ix):
    cv.line(pts, ix[name])


def tufts(cv, mask, light, ix, dark, lit, density=0.05, lens=(1, 4), seed=0):
    """grass: short strokes, longer toward the bottom of the mask (nearer); in light: dark stroke with
    a lit tip beside; in shadow: a slightly lighter stroke (the book: contrast inside the mark)."""
    r = _rng(seed)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return
    y0, y1 = ys.min(), ys.max()
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=int(len(ys) * density), replace=False):
        x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
        t = (y - y0) / max(1, y1 - y0)
        L = int(round(lens[0] + (lens[1] - lens[0]) * t * r.uniform(0.6, 1.2)))
        lean = r.choice([-0.4, 0, 0.3])
        for k in range(max(1, L)):
            X, Y = int(round(x + lean * k)), y - k
            if 0 <= Y < cv.H and 0 <= X < cv.W and mask[Y, X]:
                cv.a[Y, X] = ix[dark]
        if light is not None and light[y, x] > 0.5 and r.uniform() < 0.5:
            cv.put(x + 1, y - max(1, L) + 1, ix[lit])
Write: books/oga/places/skykomish.py
"""Place study: Skykomish, the Index Town Wall above the river forest, painted the way Oga paints.
Photo: places/skykomish_photo.jpg (Index Town Walls, SounderBruce, CC BY-SA 4.0). 320x180.

Oga's decisions for this picture (MODEL.md sections):
  [0] what it is for: a granite wall, a forest at its foot, a person could walk out of the trees.
  [7] one light, concentrated: morning sun from the upper left. A cloud shadow lies over the left
      third of the wall and the ridge forest, so the lit central wall is the point and shadow is the
      majority. Reflected light in the shadowed granite is cool (sky), loosened with warm grey.
  [6] planes: sky / ridge forest crest / the rising wall / the forest band at the foot / near maples.
      The forest band severs the wall from the foreground (p.52: severing gives depth).
  [11] air: the wall's far right end and the ridge crest are a step paler and bluer.
  [3,5] the crowd: conifers as spires (tips break every edge against the sky or the wall), the
      maples as clumps; granite as vertical facets with fall-line streaks; a sky-coloured rim on the
      far edge of the lit facets (際).
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb
from copykit import Canvas, majority
import vocab as V

W, H, OY = 320, 180, 24


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(
    sk1=c(0.66, 0.11, 252), sk2=c(0.71, 0.10, 250), sk3=c(0.76, 0.085, 248), sk4=c(0.81, 0.065, 244), sk5=c(0.86, 0.045, 238),
    # granite in light (warm pale grey) and its steps
    g1=c(0.90, 0.015, 85), g2=c(0.82, 0.018, 80), g3=c(0.73, 0.02, 70),
    # granite in shadow: cool reflected light, warm-grey loosening, dark streaks
    q1=c(0.64, 0.025, 255), q2=c(0.56, 0.028, 258), q3=c(0.48, 0.025, 255), qw=c(0.58, 0.02, 60), q4=c(0.36, 0.022, 250),
    rim=c(0.74, 0.10, 245),
    # conifers
    c_lit=c(0.56, 0.09, 135), c_mid=c(0.43, 0.07, 145), c_dark=c(0.31, 0.045, 160), c_hole=c(0.21, 0.03, 170),
    # conifers far/in shadow (aerial)
    f_lit=c(0.58, 0.05, 175), f_mid=c(0.49, 0.045, 185), f_dark=c(0.40, 0.04, 195),
    # maples
    m_cap=c(0.80, 0.15, 118), m_lit=c(0.70, 0.145, 124), m_mid=c(0.57, 0.12, 132), m_dark=c(0.42, 0.08, 140), b_hole=c(0.26, 0.045, 150),
)
NAMES = list(P); IX = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def S(pts):
    return [(x, y - OY) for x, y in pts]


RIDGE = [(0, 112), (16, 106), (40, 100), (64, 92), (88, 82), (104, 76), (120, 70), (144, 64), (168, 56), (184, 52),
         (200, 46), (216, 40), (232, 34), (248, 30), (264, 28), (280, 26), (300, 24), (320, 22)]
WALLTOP = [(0, 128), (24, 120), (48, 112), (72, 102), (96, 94), (120, 88), (144, 82), (168, 80), (192, 78), (216, 72),
           (240, 64), (264, 62), (288, 66), (304, 74), (320, 80)]
FORESTTOP = [(0, 160), (24, 162), (48, 164), (72, 160), (96, 154), (104, 146), (112, 138), (124, 134), (136, 140),
             (144, 152), (168, 152), (192, 150), (216, 148), (232, 152), (256, 156), (280, 154), (300, 146), (320, 140)]


def paint(seed=7):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=IX['sk3'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    below = lambda line: yy >= np.interp(xx, [p[0] for p in S(line)], [p[1] for p in S(line)])
    sky = ~below(RIDGE)
    ridge_forest = below(RIDGE) & ~below(WALLTOP)
    wall = below(WALLTOP) & ~below(FORESTTOP)
    forest = below(FORESTTOP)
    # the cloud shadow: a soft lobe over the left third (world-fixed in a real scene; here designed)
    cloud = (xx + 0.35 * yy + 6 * cv.noise(3.0, seed + 1)) < 118

    # ---------------- ground: masses, flat, and the light decided -----------------------------------
    V.sky_bands(cv, sky, ['sk1', 'sk2', 'sk3', 'sk4', 'sk5'], IX, 0, 60, seed=seed)
    cv.a[ridge_forest] = IX['f_mid']
    cv.a[wall & ~cloud] = IX['g2']
    cv.a[wall & cloud] = IX['q2']
    cv.a[forest] = IX['c_hole']
    cv.snapshot('1 masses and the light: sky, ridge forest, wall (lit | cloud shadow), forest')

    # ---------------- the wall: vertical facets, decided by lines ------------------------------------
    rr = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # facet boundaries: near-vertical lines leaning slightly (the granite's joints), photo x positions
    joints = [8, 30, 52, 64, 92, 112, 128, 146, 160, 176, 186, 200, 214, 232, 244, 262, 272, 286, 300, 312]
    shades = {}
    for i, (x0, x1) in enumerate(zip(joints[:-1], joints[1:])):
        lean = rr.uniform(-0.12, 0.06)
        f = (xx - x0 - lean * (yy - 60) >= 0) & (xx - x1 - lean * (yy - 60) < 0) & wall
        # each facet faces the light more or less: alternate lit/half with some shadowed recesses
        face = rr.choice(['lit', 'half', 'lit', 'recess'], p=[0.4, 0.3, 0.15, 0.15])
        if (x0 + x1) / 2 > 290:
            face = 'half'                                  # the far end, turning away: air
        shades[i] = face
        inshadow = f & cloud
        lit = f & ~cloud
        cv.a[lit] = IX[{'lit': 'g1', 'half': 'g2', 'recess': 'q1'}[face]]
        cv.a[inshadow] = IX[{'lit': 'q1', 'half': 'q2', 'recess': 'q3'}[face]]
        # uneven paint: the adjacent step in broad patches
        nxt = {'g1': 'g2', 'g2': 'g3', 'q1': 'qw', 'q2': 'q1', 'q3': 'q2'}
        for nm in set(nxt):
            m = f & (cv.a == IX[nm])
            if m.any():
                cv.mottle(m, IX[nxt[nm]], 0.22, 2.2)
        # the rim: the sky's colour down the far (right) edge of a lit facet in the sun
        if face == 'lit':
            ys = np.nonzero(lit.any(1))[0]
            for y in ys[::1]:
                row = np.nonzero(lit[y])[0]
                if len(row) and rr.uniform() < 0.8:
                    cv.put(row[-1], y, IX['rim'])
    cv.snapshot('2 the wall: vertical facets, lit/half/recess, rims on lit facets')
    # dark streaks and the great diagonal cracks (from the photo), fall-line strokes
    for pts in ([(142, 96), (146, 112), (150, 128), (152, 150)], [(176, 100), (178, 124), (180, 150)],
                [(232, 88), (234, 110), (236, 140)], [(274, 84), (278, 100), (282, 124)], [(150, 104), (162, 116), (168, 130)],
                [(40, 130), (44, 150)], [(96, 110), (98, 140)], [(206, 92), (208, 120)]):
        cv.line(S(pts), IX['q4'])
    V.fall_strokes(cv, wall & ~cloud, 'g3', IX, n=60, lens=(2, 7), drift=0.1, seed=seed)
    V.fall_strokes(cv, wall & cloud, 'q3', IX, n=40, lens=(2, 6), drift=0.1, seed=seed + 1)
    # the overhang dark on the right (photo x 272-300, y 84-110)
    cv.mass(S([(272, 90), (290, 86), (300, 96), (292, 112), (278, 108)]), IX['q3'], wet=1.0, where=wall)
    cv.snapshot('3 cracks, streaks, fall-line strokes')

    # ---------------- conifers: ridge crest against the sky (far, aerial), ledge trees, foot forest ---
    light_f = (~cloud).astype(float)
    V.conifer_mass(cv, ridge_forest | (below(RIDGE) & (yy < np.interp(xx, [p[0] for p in S(RIDGE)], [p[1] for p in S(RIDGE)]) + 3)),
                   IX, h_range=(4, 7), spacing=2.6, sun=-1, light=light_f,
                   levels={'lit': ('f_lit', 'f_dark', 'sk5'), 'shade': ('f_mid', 'f_dark', 'f_lit')}, seed=seed, fill='f_dark')
    # trees breaking the skyline: a few taller spires on the crest
    for x in range(4, 320, 7):
        y = np.interp(x, [p[0] for p in S(RIDGE)], [p[1] for p in S(RIDGE)])
        if rr.uniform() < 0.7:
            V.spire(cv, x + rr.integers(-2, 3), y + 2, rr.integers(5, 9), IX, body='f_mid', dark='f_dark', lit='f_lit', sun=-1, seed=x)
    # a few trees on ledges of the wall
    for (x, y, h) in [(60, 124, 6), (116, 116, 7), (190, 98, 5), (252, 92, 6), (300, 110, 5), (22, 140, 6)]:
        V.spire(cv, x, y - OY, h, IX, body='c_mid', dark='c_dark', lit='c_lit', sun=-1, seed=x)
    # the foot forest: big stand, lit tops where the sun reaches (right of the cloud)
    V.conifer_mass(cv, forest, IX, h_range=(8, 22), spacing=4.0, sun=-1, light=light_f, seed=seed + 3)
    cv.snapshot('4 conifers: crest (aerial), ledges, the foot forest (spires, tips break the edge)')

    # ---------------- near maples: clumps, bottom right and bottom left ------------------------------
    mright = cv.mask_poly(S([(236, 204), (244, 186), (256, 176), (272, 170), (292, 168), (312, 172), (320, 176), (320, 204)]))
    mleft = cv.mask_poly(S([(40, 204), (46, 186), (58, 174), (72, 172), (86, 180), (92, 204)]))
    lightm = gaussian_filter((xx + yy * 0.2 > 60).astype(float) * 0.6 + 0.4 * (yy < 175 - OY), 2)
    for m, lf in ((mright, np.clip(1.2 - (yy - (168 - OY)) / 40 + 0.2 * cv.noise(2), 0, 1)),
                  (mleft, np.clip(0.8 - (yy - (172 - OY)) / 40 + 0.2 * cv.noise(2), 0, 1) * 0.8)):
        V.clump_mass(cv, m, IX, lf, [(0.0, ('m_mid', 'm_dark', 'b_hole')), (0.45, ('m_lit', 'm_mid', 'm_dark')),
                                     (0.75, ('m_cap', 'm_lit', 'm_mid'))], r_range=(2.4, 3.6), step=4, seed=seed + 5)
    cv.snapshot('5 near maples: clumps, lit on the sun side')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv = paint()
    import os; os.makedirs(f'{ROOT}/places/out', exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/places/out/skykomish.png')
    photo = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/skykomish_photo.jpg')
    ph = photo.resize((320, 213), Image.LANCZOS).crop((0, OY, 320, OY + 180))
    before = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/skykomish_pair_right.png')
    grid([fit(ph, w=640), fit(before, w=640), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)], 1,
         ['photo (Index Town Walls, SounderBruce, CC BY-SA 4.0)', 'before: the place lane render (place-skykomish)', 'after: painted the Oga way, 320x180, 2x'],
         title='Skykomish: the Index wall').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_skykomish.png')
    grid([zoom(cv.pal[a], 2) for _, a in cv.stages], 2, [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='Skykomish, stage by stage').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_skykomish_stages.png')

Write: books/oga/src/painter.py
"""Painting a scene the Oga way, from a plan (masses + materials) and a light design.

This is the passage copies (copies/p1-p3) and the Skykomish study generalised: the same marks
(src/vocab.py), the same order, one function per material. Forms come from a *form source*: a photo
(for photo studies) or a sim render (for voxsim), used only for its VALUES inside each mass, organised
(smoothed) so they become shapes. Colour never comes from the source: each material gets a lit family
and a shadow family built by Oga's rules (palette_for).

    scene = Scene(W, H, form_rgb)            # form_rgb: HxWx3 float, the source at output size
    scene.add('sky', 'sky', mask)            # masses in painting order (far to near)
    scene.add('ridge', 'far', mask, key=(L,C,h), air=0.6)
    ...
    scene.light(cast=mask_of_designed_shadow, sun=-1)   # the one light (MODEL 7)
    cv = scene.paint()

Material painters and the book sections they carry:
  sky        banded celeste, paler at the horizon                          (C-0772)
  far        aerial mass: two values, sky-tinted, staggered conifer edge      (cover step 6, p.54 air)
  conifer    stand: organised notan, spires breaking the top edge            (cover step 7, P2)
  broadleaf  crowns as clumps at light levels                                (P1, C-0827)
  rock       planes from organised values; lit | shadow families; rim; fall strokes  (P3, C-0792)
  meadow     one plane with undulations; organised values; tufts that lengthen with nearness;
             top-view near, side-view far                                   (C-0095, p.53)
  water      mirror of what is painted above, one step down, horizontal dash rows of sky colour,
             clear bed in the shallows                                      (C-0792 water, cover)
  gravel     stones as a crowd: dots and 2-px lozenges, lit top / dark under, size grows near
  wood       driftwood: pale facets, lit top, dark underside
  snow       white lit, blue-grey form shadow, celeste cast shadow           (p.96)
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, binary_dilation
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab, lab_to_lch
from copykit import Canvas, majority
import vocab as V

D = np.pi / 180


def lch(L, C, h):
    return lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, h * D]))


def palette_for(key, n_lit=4, n_sh=4, warm_light=True, sky_h=245.0, spread=(0.14, 0.18)):
    """Oga's families from one key colour (L, C, hue deg) = the material's mid-lit colour.
    lit family: L up to key+spread, hue walks toward yellow (h 100) as L rises, chroma rises with L
                (unmuddied, C-0081) for chromatic materials; neutrals stay low-chroma but warm.
    shadow family: L from key-0.12 down, hue pulled toward the sky's hue (reflected light), chroma a
                touch lower; plus a warm shadow variant (the object's own colour in shadow, p.56/62)."""
    L0, C0, h0 = key
    lit = []
    for i, t in enumerate(np.linspace(-0.35, 1.0, n_lit)):
        L = L0 + spread[0] * t
        hh = h0 + ((100 - h0 + 180) % 360 - 180) * 0.35 * max(t, 0) if (warm_light and C0 > 0.03) else h0
        C = C0 * (1 + 0.25 * t) if C0 > 0.03 else C0 * (1 + 0.3 * t)
        lit.append(lch(L, C, hh))
    sh = []
    for t in np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n_sh):          # darkest first
        L = L0 - 0.10 - spread[1] * t * 1.4
        pull = 0.45 if C0 < 0.04 else 0.25
        hh = h0 + ((sky_h - h0 + 180) % 360 - 180) * pull
        C = max(0.012, C0 * (0.75 - 0.25 * t))
        sh.append(lch(L, C, hh))
    warm = lch(L0 - 0.16, max(0.02, C0 * 0.6), h0 + ((60 - h0 + 180) % 360 - 180) * 0.4)
    return dict(lit=lit, sh=sh, warm=warm)


class Scene:
    def __init__(self, W, H, form_rgb, seed=0):
        self.W, self.H, self.seed = W, H, seed
        self.form = rgb_to_oklab(np.clip(form_rgb, 0, 1))[..., 0]
        self.layers = []
        self.cast = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
        self.sun = -1
        self.sky_names = None

    def add(self, name, material, mask, key=None, **kw):
        self.layers.append(dict(name=name, mat=material, mask=mask, key=key, kw=kw))

    def light(self, cast=None, sun=-1):
        if cast is not None:
            self.cast = cast
        self.sun = sun

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ palette assembly
    def _build_palette(self):
        cols, names = [], []

        def addc(n, lab):
            names.append(n); cols.append(oklab_to_rgb(lab))
        for ly in self.layers:
            n, m, key, kw = ly['name'], ly['mat'], ly['key'], ly['kw']
            if m == 'sky':
                for i, c in enumerate(kw.get('ramp', [(0.66, 0.11, 252), (0.72, 0.10, 250), (0.78, 0.08, 247), (0.84, 0.06, 242), (0.89, 0.04, 236)])):
                    addc(f'{n}.{i}', lch(*c))
                continue
            if m == 'snow':
                for i, c in enumerate([(0.99, 0.004, 100), (0.95, 0.008, 95), (0.86, 0.02, 270), (0.78, 0.03, 280), (0.70, 0.035, 283), (0.76, 0.045, 245), (0.68, 0.05, 248)]):
                    addc(f'{n}.{i}', lch(*c))
                continue
            fam = palette_for(key, **kw.get('pal', {}))
            if kw.get('air', 0) > 0:          # aerial perspective: families pulled toward the horizon sky
                a = kw['air']; hz = lch(*kw.get('horizon', (0.86, 0.04, 238)))
                fam = {k: ([v * (1 - a) + hz * a for v in fam[k]] if isinstance(fam[k], list) else fam[k] * (1 - a) + hz * a) for k in fam}
            for i, c in enumerate(fam['lit']):
                addc(f'{n}.l{i}', c)
            for i, c in enumerate(fam['sh']):
                addc(f'{n}.s{i}', c)
            addc(f'{n}.w', fam['warm'])
            if m in ('rock', 'wood', 'water'):
                addc(f'{n}.rim', lch(*kw.get('rim', (0.74, 0.10, 245))))
        self.names = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(names)}
        return np.array(cols)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ paint
    def paint(self):
        pal = self._build_palette()
        cv = Canvas(self.W, self.H, pal, seed=self.seed)
        cv.names = self.names
        self.cv = cv
        for ly in self.layers:
            getattr(self, '_' + ly['mat'])(cv, ly)
            cv.snapshot(f"{ly['name']} ({ly['mat']})")
        return cv

    # organised values of the form source inside a mask, as quantile levels
    def levels(self, mask, qs, sigma=1.0):
        Ls = gaussian_filter(self.form, sigma)
        if mask.sum() < 4:
            return np.zeros(mask.shape, int)
        t = np.quantile(Ls[mask], qs)
        return np.searchsorted(t, Ls)

    def ix(self, ly):
        n = ly['name']
        return {k.split('.', 1)[1]: v for k, v in self.names.items() if k.startswith(n + '.')}

    # ---- materials ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _sky(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly)
        ys = np.nonzero(m.any(1))[0]
        V.sky_bands(cv, m, [str(i) for i in range(len(ix))], ix, ys.min(), ys.max() * ly['kw'].get('reach', 0.9), seed=self.seed)

    def _lit_mask(self, ly, lev_lit):
        """lit where the organised form says lit AND the designed light reaches (not in cast shadow)."""
        return (lev_lit) & ~self.cast & ~ly['kw'].get('shadow', np.zeros_like(self.cast))

    def _far(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.5], 1.2)
        lit = self._lit_mask(ly, lev >= 1)
        cv.a[m] = ix['s3']
        cv.a[m & lit] = ix['l1']
        cv.a[m & lit & (self.levels(m, [0.85], 1.0) >= 1)] = ix['l2']
        if kw.get('edge') == 'conifer':
            top = [(x, int(np.argmax(m[:, x]))) for x in range(self.W) if m[:, x].any()]
            V.stagger(cv, top, 's2', ix, lens=(1, 3), group=(2, 5), gap=(1, 3), up=True, seed=self.seed)
            for k in range(3, 60, 4):
                V.stagger(cv, [(x, y + k) for x, y in top], 's2', ix, lens=(1, 2), group=(1, 3), gap=(3, 7), up=False, seed=self.seed + k)
            cv.a[~m & np.isin(cv.a, [ix['s2']]) & (np.mgrid[0:self.H, 0:self.W][0] > max(y for _, y in top) + 60)] = ix['s3']

    def _conifer(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        rr = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 3)
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.4, 0.75], 0.8)
        cv.a[m] = np.array([ix['s3'], ix['s2'], ix['s1']])[lev][m]
        lit = self._lit_mask(ly, lev >= 2)
        cv.a[m & lit] = ix['l0']
        cv.a = majority(cv.a, 1)
        hr = kw.get('h', (6, 16))
        top = [(x, int(np.argmax(m[:, x]))) for x in range(self.W) if m[:, x].any()]
        topd = dict(top)
        spacing = kw.get('spacing', 3)
        for x in range(min(topd), max(topd) + 1, spacing):
            if x not in topd:
                continue
            h = int(rr.integers(*hr))
            y0 = topd[x]
            litt = not self.cast[min(y0, self.H - 1), x]
            V.spire(cv, x + rr.uniform(-1, 1), y0 + h * 0.6, h, ix, body='s1' if litt else 's2', dark='s3',
                    lit='l1' if litt else 's1', sun=self.sun, seed=x * 3)
        pk = m & lit & (gaussian_filter(self.form, 0.8) > np.quantile(self.form[m], 0.85))
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(pk)
        for i in rr.choice(len(ys), size=min(kw.get('n_inner', 40), len(ys)), replace=False):
            h = int(rr.integers(hr[0], max(hr[0] + 1, hr[1] // 2 + 1)))
            V.spire(cv, xs[i], ys[i] + h * 0.5, h, ix, body='l0', dark='s2', lit='l2', sun=self.sun, seed=int(i), where=m)

    def _broadleaf(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        Ls = gaussian_filter(self.form, 1.0)
        lo, hi = np.quantile(Ls[m], [0.05, 0.95]) if m.any() else (0, 1)
        light = np.clip((Ls - lo) / (hi - lo + 1e-9), 0, 1)
        light = np.where(self.cast, light * 0.45, light)
        V.clump_mass(cv, m, ix, light, [(0.0, ('s2', 's1', 's3')), (0.45, ('l0', 's0', 's2')),
                                        (0.62, ('l1', 'l0', 's1')), (0.8, ('l2', 'l1', 's1'))],
                     r_range=kw.get('r', (2.0, 3.4)), step=kw.get('step', 4), hole='s3', seed=self.seed + 5)

    def _rock(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.3, 0.55, 0.82], kw.get('sigma', 1.0))
        litz = ~self.cast & ~kw.get('shadow', np.zeros_like(m))
        lit_names = np.array([ix['s2'], ix['l0'], ix['l1'], ix['l2']])
        sh_names = np.array([ix['s0'], ix['s1'], ix['s2'], ix['s3']])
        cv.a[m & litz] = lit_names[lev][m & litz]
        cv.a[m & ~litz] = sh_names[lev][m & ~litz]
        cv.a = majority(cv.a, 1)
        cv.mottle(m & ~litz & (cv.a == ix['s3']), ix['w'], 0.3, 2.0)
        litm = m & litz & np.isin(cv.a, [ix['l1'], ix['l2']])
        dk = np.roll(np.isin(cv.a, [ix['s0'], ix['s1'], ix['s2']]), -1, 0)
        rr = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 9)
        cv.a[litm & dk & (rr.uniform(size=m.shape) < kw.get('rim_p', 0.5))] = ix['rim']
        V.fall_strokes(cv, litm, 'l0', ix, n=int(m.sum() * 0.004) + 3, lens=(2, 6), drift=kw.get('drift', 0.1), seed=self.seed)

    def _meadow(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.3, 0.6, 0.85], kw.get('sigma', 1.6))
        litz = ~self.cast & ~kw.get('shadow', np.zeros_like(m))
        cv.a[m & litz] = np.array([ix['s3'], ix['l0'], ix['l1'], ix['l2']])[lev][m & litz]
        cv.a[m & ~litz] = np.array([ix['s0'], ix['s1'], ix['s2'], ix['s3']])[lev][m & ~litz]
        cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
        light = (litz & (lev >= 1)).astype(float)
        V.tufts(cv, m & litz, light, ix, 's3', 'l3', density=kw.get('tuft', 0.05), lens=kw.get('lens', (1, 5)), seed=self.seed)
        V.tufts(cv, m & ~litz, None, ix, 's0', 's3', density=kw.get('tuft', 0.05) * 0.6, lens=kw.get('lens', (1, 5)), seed=self.seed + 1)
        for (col, n, where) in kw.get('flowers', []):
            k = self.names[col] if isinstance(col, str) else col
            rr = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 11)
            ys, xs = np.nonzero(m & where)
            if len(ys):
                for i in rr.choice(len(ys), size=min(n, len(ys)), replace=False):
                    cv.put(xs[i], ys[i], k)

    def _water(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        H, W = self.H, self.W
        line = kw['line']              # waterline row per column (array W)
        src = cv.a.copy()
        rr = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 13)
        # 1 the water's own colour as base (dark, sky tinted)
        cv.a[m] = ix['s2']
        # 2 the mirror: copy the painted pixel above the waterline, mapped one step down
        down = kw.get('down', {})
        for x in range(W):
            y0 = int(line[x])
            for y in range(y0, H):
                if not m[y, x]:
                    continue
                ys = int(round(y0 - (y - y0) * kw.get('squash', 1.0)))
                if 0 <= ys < H and not m[ys, x]:
                    v = src[ys, x]
                    cv.a[y, x] = down.get(int(v), v)
        # 3 horizontal dash rows: sky-coloured sheen where the surface faces the sky, broken
        n = gaussian_filter(rr.standard_normal((H, W)), (0.3, 2.5))
        n = (n - n.mean()) / n.std()
        dash = m & (n > kw.get('dash_t', 1.1))
        cv.a[dash] = ix['rim']
        dk = m & (n < -1.4)
        cv.a[dk] = ix['s3']
        # 4 shallows: the bed shows through near the shore (a band below the line)
        if kw.get('bed'):
            bed_mask = kw['bed'] & m
            cv.a[bed_mask & (rr.uniform(size=(H, W)) < 0.5)] = ix['w']

    def _gravel(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly); kw = ly['kw']
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.35, 0.7], 1.5)
        litz = ~self.cast
        cv.a[m & litz] = np.array([ix['s3'], ix['l0'], ix['l1']])[lev][m & litz]
        cv.a[m & ~litz] = np.array([ix['s1'], ix['s2'], ix['s3']])[lev][m & ~litz]
        rr = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 17)
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m)
        if len(ys) == 0:
            return
        y0, y1 = ys.min(), ys.max()
        for i in rr.choice(len(ys), size=int(len(ys) * kw.get('density', 0.12)), replace=False):
            x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
            t = (y - y0) / max(1, y1 - y0)
            w = 1 + int(t * 2.5 * rr.uniform(0.5, 1.2))          # stones grow nearer
            lit = litz[y, x]
            top = ix['l2'] if lit else ix['s3']
            under = ix['s0']
            for k in range(w):
                cv.put(x + k, y, top if rr.uniform() < 0.8 else ix['l0'])
            if w > 1 or rr.uniform() < 0.5:
                for k in range(w):
                    cv.put(x + k, y + 1, under)

    def _wood(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly)
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.35, 0.7], 0.7)
        litz = ~self.cast
        cv.a[m & litz] = np.array([ix['s2'], ix['l1'], ix['l3']])[lev][m & litz]
        cv.a[m & ~litz] = np.array([ix['s0'], ix['s1'], ix['s3']])[lev][m & ~litz]
        under = m & ~np.roll(m, -1, 0)
        cv.a[under] = ix['s0']
        top = m & ~np.roll(m, 1, 0) & litz
        cv.a[top] = ix['l3']

    def _snow(self, cv, ly):
        m = ly['mask']; ix = self.ix(ly)
        lev = self.levels(m, [0.3, 0.6], 1.0)
        litz = ~self.cast
        cv.a[m & litz] = np.array([ix['2'], ix['1'], ix['0']])[lev][m & litz]
        cv.a[m & ~litz] = np.array([ix['6'], ix['5'], ix['5']])[lev][m & ~litz]

"""Place study: the Enchantments, Prusik Peak over a tarn with larches, painted the Oga way. 200x300.
Photo: places/enchant_photo.jpg ('Gnome tarn on ice', openverse; see places/refs/*/sources.tsv).

Oga's decisions:
  [7] one light, concentrated: the sun on the peak's face and the larch band; the whole near shore
      and water lie in the shadow of the ridge behind the viewer (shadow ~ 2/3, C-0792). The left
      shoulder of the peak turns away and is in shadow too.
  [6] planes: sky / peak (rising) / larch and boulder shore (the severing band, p.52) / the tarn
      (lying plane, mirror) / near boulders and tussocks.
  [10] shadow colour: the shaded shore keeps its colours (tussock ochre, granite warm grey) at low
      value; the water in shadow shows the bed (clear water, C-0792).
  [1] the one saturated note: the larch gold, in light. Everything else near-neutral.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from painter import Scene, lch
from copykit import Canvas

W, H = 200, 300


def poly(cv, pts):
    return cv.mask_poly(pts)


def paint(seed=11):
    photo = np.asarray(Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/enchant_photo.jpg').convert('RGB').resize((W, H), Image.BOX), float) / 255
    tmp = Canvas(W, H, np.zeros((1, 3)))
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    peak = poly(tmp, [(0, 90), (10, 78), (24, 72), (40, 74), (48, 64), (60, 56), (72, 44), (80, 34), (88, 22), (92, 24),
                      (98, 40), (108, 46), (116, 62), (126, 70), (140, 70), (152, 72), (168, 78), (184, 84), (200, 94), (200, 128), (0, 128)])
    shoulder = poly(tmp, [(0, 90), (10, 78), (24, 72), (40, 74), (50, 100), (46, 128), (0, 128)])
    larch = poly(tmp, [(0, 104), (16, 98), (30, 96), (40, 104), (60, 112), (76, 118), (100, 112), (130, 108), (150, 104), (170, 108),
                       (190, 112), (200, 110), (200, 132), (0, 132)])
    shore = poly(tmp, [(0, 118), (30, 116), (60, 122), (100, 126), (140, 126), (170, 124), (200, 126), (200, 140), (150, 136),
                       (100, 136), (50, 142), (0, 150)])
    water = (yy >= 132) & ~shore | (yy >= 150)
    line = np.array([np.argmax(water[:, x]) for x in range(W)])
    boulders = [poly(tmp, p) for p in (
        [(0, 196), (8, 194), (18, 198), (20, 214), (10, 220), (0, 218)], [(14, 200), (26, 196), (36, 200), (38, 214), (26, 220), (16, 216)],
        [(70, 206), (84, 200), (98, 196), (112, 194), (122, 204), (118, 218), (96, 222), (78, 218)],
        [(166, 204), (178, 200), (194, 204), (190, 212), (172, 212)], [(88, 240), (100, 238), (116, 244), (112, 252), (92, 250)],
        [(70, 206), (80, 202), (88, 206), (86, 212), (74, 212)], [(0, 150), (20, 148), (40, 150), (34, 158), (10, 160), (0, 158)],
        [(170, 132), (186, 128), (200, 130), (200, 138), (180, 140)])]
    tuss = [poly(tmp, p) for p in ([(0, 190), (16, 186), (40, 190), (52, 200), (40, 206), (18, 204), (0, 206)],
                                   [(30, 244), (48, 238), (66, 242), (70, 256), (60, 272), (40, 276), (30, 264)])]
    sky = ~peak & (yy < 128)
    rock_all = peak & ~larch
    sc = Scene(W, H, photo, seed=seed)
    sc.add('sky', 'sky', sky, ramp=[(0.50, 0.14, 262), (0.57, 0.13, 258), (0.64, 0.11, 254), (0.71, 0.09, 250), (0.78, 0.07, 245), (0.84, 0.05, 240)])
    sc.add('peak', 'rock', rock_all & ~shoulder, key=(0.80, 0.012, 80), shadow=np.zeros((H, W), bool), rim_p=0.35, drift=-0.15)
    sc.add('shoulder', 'rock', rock_all & shoulder, key=(0.66, 0.012, 80), shadow=np.ones((H, W), bool), rim_p=0.0)
    sc.add('larch', 'broadleaf', larch & ~shore, key=(0.74, 0.14, 82), r=(1.4, 2.0), step=3)
    sc.add('shore', 'rock', shore, key=(0.84, 0.01, 90), rim_p=0.2)
    sc.add('tarn', 'water', water & ~np.any(boulders, 0) & ~np.any(tuss, 0), key=(0.40, 0.03, 225), line=line,
           squash=1.15, bed=(yy > 205), dash_t=1.3, rim=(0.66, 0.08, 250))
    for i, b in enumerate(boulders):
        sc.add(f'b{i}', 'rock', b, key=(0.80, 0.012, 80), rim_p=0.4)
    for i, t in enumerate(tuss):
        sc.add(f't{i}', 'meadow', t, key=(0.66, 0.08, 78), tuft=0.25, lens=(1, 4))
    # the designed light: everything below the larch band is in the ridge's shadow; so is the shoulder
    sc.light(cast=(yy > 132) | shoulder, sun=+1)
    cv = sc.paint()
    return cv, sc

        """index -> the palette entry nearest to 'one step down' (L - dl, slightly less chroma)."""
        lab = rgb_to_oklab(cv.pal)
        tgt = lab * np.array([1, 0.85, 0.85]) - np.array([dl, 0, 0])

    def _water(self, cv, ly):

        feet), horizontal breaks between them; sky-coloured dash rows only in the mirror part."""

                ys = int(round(y0 - 1 - d * kw.get('squash', 1.0)))

                cv.a[y0, x] = ix['s0']

"""Place study: the Olympic coast, driftwood on the gravel at Rialto Beach under a coastal forest. 320x172.
Photo: places/olycoast_photo.jpg ('Driftwood along Rialto Beach 2022', Sea Cow, CC BY-SA 4.0).

Oga's decisions:
  [4] an overcast sea-coast day: the tone range is narrow, so the range lives in coloured greys
      (C39-5, p.103): violet-grey sky, green-grey forest, warm brown-grey gravel.
  [7] light concentrated anyway: a break in the cloud lets one pale beam onto the driftwood root and
      the gravel around it (C-0022: 'one thin ray gives awe'). Forest and far beach stay in shadow.
  [6] planes: sky / the forest wall (tips break the sky) / the beach-grass band (severing band) /
      the gravel plane (stones grow toward us) / the driftwood (the point).
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, binary_opening
from okl import rgb_to_oklab
from painter import Scene
from copykit import Canvas

W, H = 320, 172


def paint(seed=21):
    photo = np.asarray(Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/olycoast_photo.jpg').convert('RGB').resize((W, H), Image.BOX), float) / 255
    L = gaussian_filter(rgb_to_oklab(photo)[..., 0], 0.8)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    t = Canvas(W, H, np.zeros((1, 3)))
    sky = (L > 0.74) & (yy < 75)
    sky = binary_opening(sky, iterations=1)
    wood = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    for p in ([(112, 140), (118, 132), (150, 126), (180, 120), (206, 114), (212, 122), (184, 130), (150, 136), (120, 144)],
              [(206, 100), (224, 96), (246, 100), (264, 106), (270, 120), (266, 146), (252, 156), (230, 152), (212, 140), (204, 120)],
              [(186, 112), (196, 100), (206, 88), (212, 90), (204, 104), (196, 114)], [(216, 92), (222, 78), (226, 80), (222, 96)],
              [(54, 150), (70, 144), (88, 140), (90, 144), (72, 150), (58, 154)], [(160, 106), (200, 102), (240, 104), (300, 104), (318, 108), (300, 110), (240, 110), (170, 112)],
              [(0, 116), (20, 114), (40, 116), (20, 118), (0, 119)]):
        wood |= t.mask_poly(p)
    grassband = (yy >= 84) & (yy < 104) & ~sky
    forest = (yy < 96) & ~sky & ~grassband
    gravel = (yy >= 96) & ~wood & ~grassband | ((yy >= 104) & ~wood)
    grassband &= ~wood
    sc = Scene(W, H, photo, seed=seed)
    sc.add('sky', 'sky', sky, ramp=[(0.72, 0.012, 300), (0.77, 0.012, 295), (0.82, 0.01, 285), (0.87, 0.008, 270), (0.91, 0.006, 250)], reach=1.4)
    sc.add('forest', 'conifer', forest, key=(0.46, 0.04, 165), h=(8, 22), spacing=4, n_inner=20)
    sc.add('grass', 'meadow', grassband, key=(0.62, 0.07, 125), tuft=0.12, lens=(1, 4))
    sc.add('gravel', 'gravel', gravel, key=(0.58, 0.012, 60), density=0.16)
    sc.add('wood', 'wood', wood, key=(0.80, 0.02, 75))
    # the beam: an oval of light on the driftwood and gravel around it
    beam = (((xx - 200) / 95.0) ** 2 + ((yy - 128) / 34.0) ** 2) < 1
    beam = beam | (((xx - 200) / 60.0) ** 2 + ((yy - 110) / 30.0) ** 2 < 1) & wood
    sc.light(cast=~beam, sun=+1)
    return sc.paint(), sc


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv, sc = paint()
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/places/out/olycoast.png')
    photo = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/olycoast_photo.jpg').resize((W, H), Image.LANCZOS)
    before = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/olycoast_pair_right.png')
    grid([zoom(photo, 2), fit(before, w=640), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)], 1,
         ['photo (Rialto Beach driftwood, Sea Cow, CC BY-SA 4.0)', 'a place-olycoast lane render (different view, for the current look)', 'painted the Oga way, 320x172, 2x'],
         title='Olympic coast: driftwood at Rialto').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_olycoast.png')

"""Place study: the Yakima River canyon, dry hills over a willow-lined river. 320x180.
Photo: places/yakima_photo.jpg ('Umptanum Ridge water gap', Wikimedia Commons; sources.tsv).

Oga's decisions:
  [11] dry interior air, low humidity: good nuke; the far ridge is crisp, only a step bluer.
  [7] the sun from the left and behind: the big grass ramp is the lit plane; the near-left slope and
      the right shoulder (under a cloud shadow) are in shadow. Light concentrated on the ramp.
  [6] planes: sky / far ridge / the lit ramp and dark slopes / the terrace (flat, lying) / the willow
      band (severs the river from the hills) / the river (mirror) / near dry grass.
  [4][10] shadows full of colour: the shaded slope is violet-brown, not grey; the willows are
      red-brown in winter; the river mirrors them one step down.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, binary_opening
from okl import rgb_to_oklab, lab_to_lch
from painter import Scene
from copykit import Canvas

W, H, OY = 320, 180, 16


def paint(seed=31):
    photo = np.asarray(Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/yakima_photo.jpg').convert('RGB').resize((W, 213), Image.BOX), float)[OY:OY + H] / 255
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(photo); L = gaussian_filter(lab[..., 0], 0.8)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    t = Canvas(W, H, np.zeros((1, 3)))
    S = lambda pts: [(x, y - OY) for x, y in pts]
    hill_line = t.mask_poly(S([(0, 16), (0, 50), (40, 44), (100, 32), (160, 18), (200, 6), (224, 0 + 16), (224, 16)]))
    sky = (yy < 60) & ~(yy >= np.interp(xx, [0, 40, 100, 160, 200, 222, 320], np.array([50, 44, 32, 18, 6, 0, -10]) - OY))
    far = t.mask_poly(S([(0, 50), (40, 44), (100, 32), (150, 22), (140, 34), (90, 44), (40, 56), (0, 64)])) & ~sky
    bank = np.interp(xx, [0, 320], [110 - OY, 125 - OY])
    hills = ~sky & ~far & (yy < 78 - OY)
    terrace = (yy >= 78 - OY) & (yy < 96 - OY)
    willow = (yy >= 92 - OY) & (yy < bank) & ~terrace | ((yy >= 96 - OY) & (yy < bank))
    near = t.mask_poly(S([(0, 170), (60, 168), (120, 172), (200, 176), (250, 190), (262, 213), (0, 213)]))
    river = (yy >= bank) & ~near
    line = np.array([int(np.argmax(river[:, x])) if river[:, x].any() else H for x in range(W)])
    sc = Scene(W, H, photo, seed=seed)
    sc.add('sky', 'sky', sky, ramp=[(0.72, 0.08, 250), (0.78, 0.065, 247), (0.84, 0.05, 244), (0.90, 0.03, 240)], reach=1.6)
    sc.add('far', 'far', far, key=(0.62, 0.03, 60), air=0.35, horizon=(0.84, 0.04, 245))
    sc.add('hills', 'meadow', hills, key=(0.72, 0.06, 80), tuft=0.03, lens=(1, 2), sigma=2.0,
           pal=dict(sky_h=300))                           # shadowed slopes go violet-brown
    sc.add('terrace', 'meadow', terrace, key=(0.70, 0.05, 85), tuft=0.06, lens=(1, 2))
    sc.add('willow', 'broadleaf', willow, key=(0.55, 0.07, 40), r=(1.6, 2.4), step=3)
    sc.add('river', 'water', river, key=(0.42, 0.03, 200), line=line, refl_len=400, squash=1.0, dash_t=1.4,
           rim=(0.80, 0.05, 240))
    sc.add('near', 'meadow', near, key=(0.74, 0.07, 78), tuft=0.35, lens=(2, 7), sigma=1.2)
    cloud = (xx > 236) & (yy < 70 - OY) | (xx < 70) & (yy < 80 - OY)
    sc.light(cast=cloud, sun=-1)
    return sc.paint(), sc


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv, sc = paint()
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/places/out/yakima.png')
    photo = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/yakima_photo.jpg').resize((W, 213), Image.LANCZOS).crop((0, OY, W, OY + H))
    before = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/yakima_pair_right.png')
    grid([zoom(photo, 2), fit(before, w=640), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)], 1,
         ['photo (Umptanum Ridge water gap, Yakima River, Wikimedia Commons)', 'a place-yakima lane render (evening)', 'painted the Oga way, 320x180, 2x'],
         title='Yakima canyon').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_yakima.png')

"""Place study: the Yakima River canyon, dry hills over a willow-lined river. 320x180. Hand-designed.
Photo: places/yakima_photo.jpg (Umptanum Ridge water gap, Wikimedia Commons; see places/refs sources.tsv)

(The first version ran this through the generic painter, src/painter.py: rectangular cloud boxes, the
hills mirrored in a river 30 m wide, willow noise. Kept as a lesson in LESSONS.md.)

Oga's decisions, plane by plane (MODEL sections):
  sky        3 flat bands of pale celeste, a flat white cloud lobe (low humidity, clear: [11])
  far ridge  a crisp far range, sky-tinted violet with snow patches, 2 values ([11] air, p.54)
  left slope turned from the sun: in shadow, violet-brown, a few gully lines ([7] shadow colour)
  the ramp   the lit plane, warm straw, graded by a hake from the lit crest down ([5] hake);
             the rimrock band: a row of dark basalt facets with lit tops (the 'clearly shaped thing'
             that lets the vague grass read as a huge slope, C-0064 p.52)
  shoulder   under a cloud shadow whose edge follows the slope's fall line, violet ([7], p.56)
  terrace    a flat lying plane, pale; a fence line and one pine for scale
  willows    the severing band ([6], p.52): red-brown winter stems as vertical strokes, lit tips,
             a gold fringe of dry grass at the cut bank
  river      short mirror of the band, one step down; then the water's own green-grey; the sky's
             light sheen on the left; horizontal dash rows; the drift boat as the one point
  near grass dry straw tufts, long blades lit, dark gaps (top view near, p.53)
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority
import vocab as V

W, H = 320, 180


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(
    sk0=c(0.76, 0.07, 250), sk1=c(0.82, 0.055, 246), sk2=c(0.88, 0.035, 240), cl=c(0.96, 0.008, 230), cl2=c(0.90, 0.012, 250),
    fr1=c(0.72, 0.03, 290), fr2=c(0.64, 0.035, 295), sn=c(0.93, 0.01, 250),
    ls1=c(0.42, 0.035, 330), ls2=c(0.50, 0.035, 340), ls3=c(0.35, 0.03, 320),          # the shaded slope
    ra1=c(0.86, 0.06, 88), ra2=c(0.80, 0.065, 82), ra3=c(0.74, 0.065, 76), ra4=c(0.67, 0.06, 70),  # the lit ramp
    rk1=c(0.30, 0.025, 50), rk2=c(0.62, 0.05, 70),                                      # basalt, lit top
    sb=c(0.55, 0.045, 110),                                                              # sage dots
    sh1=c(0.56, 0.04, 320), sh2=c(0.50, 0.04, 315),                                      # cloud shadow on the ramp
    te1=c(0.80, 0.05, 90), te2=c(0.72, 0.05, 88), fence=c(0.45, 0.03, 70),
    pn1=c(0.38, 0.05, 150), pn2=c(0.26, 0.035, 160),
    wi1=c(0.62, 0.07, 30), wi2=c(0.52, 0.07, 25), wi3=c(0.42, 0.06, 20), wi4=c(0.33, 0.04, 20), wit=c(0.74, 0.06, 40),
    go1=c(0.80, 0.11, 78), go2=c(0.68, 0.10, 70), bank=c(0.40, 0.04, 60),
    wa1=c(0.46, 0.035, 190), wa2=c(0.40, 0.035, 195), wa3=c(0.34, 0.03, 200), wsky=c(0.72, 0.03, 230), wsky2=c(0.62, 0.03, 220),
    wdash=c(0.80, 0.03, 235),
    boat=c(0.35, 0.03, 200), red=c(0.55, 0.14, 25), blu=c(0.45, 0.10, 255),
    ng1=c(0.86, 0.06, 85), ng2=c(0.76, 0.07, 80), ng3=c(0.62, 0.06, 70), ng4=c(0.42, 0.04, 60),
)
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def line_y(pts, xx):
    return np.interp(xx, [p[0] for p in pts], [p[1] for p in pts])


def paint(seed=31):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['sk1'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    SKYLINE = [(0, 34), (40, 28), (100, 16), (160, 4), (200, -6), (320, -20)]
    FAR_BASE = [(0, 50), (40, 44), (80, 38), (120, 30), (150, 22), (165, 16)]
    RAMP_TOP = [(60, 60), (100, 44), (140, 28), (180, 12), (210, 0), (320, -10)]
    TERRACE = [(0, 64), (60, 64), (120, 62), (200, 60), (260, 58), (320, 58)]
    BAND_TOP = [(0, 76), (60, 78), (120, 80), (200, 80), (260, 80), (320, 82)]
    BANK = [(0, 94), (80, 98), (160, 101), (240, 104), (320, 108)]
    sky = yy < line_y(SKYLINE, xx)
    # ---- sky: bands and a flat cloud lobe
    V.sky_bands(cv, sky, ['sk0', 'sk1', 'sk2'], I, 0, 40, seed=seed)
    cv.mass([(0, 0), (60, 0), (110, 4), (90, 10), (40, 14), (0, 18)], I['cl'], wet=1.5, where=sky)
    cv.mass([(0, 12), (40, 12), (70, 8), (50, 16), (10, 22)], I['cl2'], wet=1.0, where=sky)
    # ---- far ridge
    far = ~sky & (yy < line_y(FAR_BASE, xx)) & (xx < 170)
    cv.a[far] = I['fr1']
    cv.a[far & (yy > line_y(SKYLINE, xx) + 6)] = I['fr2']
    for (x, y) in [(60, 26), (72, 24), (90, 20), (104, 18), (118, 16), (130, 14)]:
        cv.mass([(x, y), (x + 5, y - 1), (x + 3, y + 1)], I['sn'], where=far)
    # ---- left slope (shadow) and the lit ramp
    slope = ~sky & ~far & (yy < line_y(TERRACE, xx))
    ramp = slope & (yy < line_y(RAMP_TOP, xx) + (xx - 60) * 0.0) & False
    lit_ramp = slope & ((yy - line_y(RAMP_TOP, xx)) > 0) & (xx > 70)
    shaded = slope & ~lit_ramp
    cv.a[shaded] = I['ls1']
    cv.mottle(shaded, I['ls2'], 0.25, 3.0)
    for pts in ([(20, 44), (30, 56), (36, 62)], [(48, 40), (52, 52), (58, 62)], [(8, 50), (12, 62)]):
        cv.line(pts, I['ls3'])
    # the ramp: a hake gradation from the lit crest (upper left) down and right
    V_ = ((xx - 90) * 0.5 + (yy - 20) * 1.0)
    t = np.clip(V_ / 120, 0, 0.999) * 4
    q = np.clip(np.floor(t + 0.6 * cv.noise(1.2) * ((t % 1 < 0.25) | (t % 1 > 0.75))), 0, 3).astype(int)
    cv.a[lit_ramp] = np.array([I['ra1'], I['ra2'], I['ra3'], I['ra4']])[q][lit_ramp]
    # the rimrock: a row of dark basalt facets with a lit top line, following the slope contour
    for x0 in range(176, 320, 7):
        y0 = 30 + (x0 - 176) * 0.02 + r.integers(-1, 2)
        w = int(r.integers(4, 7)); h = int(r.integers(3, 6))
        cv.mass([(x0, y0), (x0 + w, y0 + 1), (x0 + w - 1, y0 + h), (x0 + 1, y0 + h - 1)], I['rk1'], where=slope)
        cv.line([(x0, y0), (x0 + w, y0 + 1)], I['rk2'])
    cv.line([(176, 36), (320, 40)], I['rk1'])
    # sage dots scattered on the lit ramp, sparser uphill (far)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(lit_ramp)
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=110, replace=False):
        if r.uniform() < (ys[i] / 60):
            cv.dab(xs[i], ys[i], I['sb'], 'dot' if ys[i] < 40 else 'h2')
    # the shoulder in a cloud shadow whose edge follows the fall line (diagonal, soft)
    cloud = lit_ramp & ((xx - 250) - (yy - 10) * 0.9 + 5 * cv.noise(2.5) > 0)
    cv.a[cloud & np.isin(cv.a, [I['ra1'], I['ra2']])] = I['sh1']
    cv.a[cloud & np.isin(cv.a, [I['ra3'], I['ra4'], I['sb']])] = I['sh2']
    cv.snapshot('1 sky, far ridge, the shaded slope, the lit ramp (hake), rimrock, cloud shadow')
    # ---- terrace: flat, pale; fence line; one pine
    terr = ~sky & (yy >= line_y(TERRACE, xx)) & (yy < line_y(BAND_TOP, xx))
    cv.a[terr] = I['te1']
    cv.mottle(terr, I['te2'], 0.35, 2.5)
    cv.line([(0, 69), (120, 67), (320, 64)], I['fence'])
    for x in range(4, 320, 9):
        cv.put(x, int(round(69 - x * 0.0156)) - 1, I['fence'])
    V.spire(cv, 30, 86, 26, {'b': I['pn1'], 'd': I['pn2'], 'l': I['pn1']}, body='b', dark='d', lit='l', sun=-1, w=4)
    V.spire(cv, 44, 80, 12, {'b': I['pn1'], 'd': I['pn2'], 'l': I['pn1']}, body='b', dark='d', lit='l', sun=-1, w=3)
    cv.snapshot('2 terrace, fence, pines')
    # ---- the willow band: vertical strokes, lit tips, gold fringe at the bank
    band = (yy >= line_y(BAND_TOP, xx)) & (yy < line_y(BANK, xx))
    cv.a[band] = I['wi3']
    for x in range(W):
        top = int(np.ceil(line_y(BAND_TOP, x))) + int(2 * np.sin(x * 0.7) + r.integers(-1, 2))
        bot = int(line_y(BANK, x))
        for y in range(max(0, top), bot):
            k = (y - top) / max(1, bot - top)
            v = r.uniform()
            idx = I['wi1'] if v < 0.3 - 0.2 * k else (I['wi2'] if v < 0.65 else (I['wi3'] if v < 0.9 else I['wi4']))
            if (x + y // 2) % 3 == 0:
                idx = I['wi4'] if k > 0.3 else I['wi2']        # vertical stem gaps
            cv.a[y, x] = idx
        if top >= 0 and r.uniform() < 0.6:
            cv.put(x, top - 1, I['wit'])                         # stem tips breaking the top edge
            if r.uniform() < 0.4:
                cv.put(x, top - 2, I['wit'])
        # gold fringe: the last 4-6 rows above the bank
        gl = int(r.integers(3, 7))
        for y in range(bot - gl, bot):
            cv.put(x, y, I['go1'] if (y - (bot - gl)) < gl // 2 or r.uniform() < 0.3 else I['go2'])
        cv.put(x, bot, I['bank'])
    cv.snapshot('3 willow band: vertical stems, lit tips, gold fringe, dark cut bank')
    # ---- river
    river = (yy > line_y(BANK, xx))
    near = cv.mask_poly([(0, 150), (60, 148), (120, 152), (200, 156), (250, 168), (262, 180), (0, 180)])
    river &= ~near
    src = cv.a.copy()
    down = {I['go1']: I['go2'], I['go2']: I['bank'], I['wi1']: I['wi2'], I['wi2']: I['wi3'], I['wi3']: I['wi4'],
            I['wi4']: I['wa3'], I['wit']: I['wi2'], I['bank']: I['wa3'], I['te1']: I['wa1'], I['te2']: I['wa1']}
    for x in range(W):
        b = int(line_y(BANK, x))
        for y in range(b + 1, H):
            if not river[y, x]:
                continue
            d = y - b
            ys = b - d                                            # mirror about the bank line
            if d < 20 and ys > line_y(BAND_TOP, x) - 2:
                cv.a[y, x] = down.get(int(src[ys, x]), I['wa2'])
            else:
                cv.a[y, x] = I['wa2']
    # the water's own depth: lighter where the sky is reflected (left, far), darker near/right
    wz = river & (np.arange(H)[:, None] > line_y(BANK, xx) + 18)
    zone = (xx * 0.7 + (yy - 100) * 1.6 + 25 * cv.noise(4.0))
    cv.a[wz & (zone < 60)] = I['wsky']
    cv.a[wz & (zone >= 60) & (zone < 90)] = I['wsky2']
    cv.a[wz & (zone >= 90) & (zone < 200)] = I['wa1']
    cv.a[wz & (zone >= 260)] = I['wa3']
    # horizontal dash rows (ripples), more toward us
    n = gaussian_filter(r.standard_normal((H, W)), (0.3, 3.0)); n /= n.std()
    cv.a[wz & (n > 1.5)] = I['wdash']
    cv.a[wz & (n < -1.6)] = I['wa3']
    # the boat: the one point that gives the scale (C-0773)
    cv.mass([(194, 142), (208, 142), (206, 144), (196, 144)], I['boat'])
    cv.put(198, 140, I['red']); cv.put(198, 141, I['red']); cv.put(202, 140, I['blu']); cv.put(202, 141, I['blu'])
    cv.line([(190, 145), (212, 145)], I['wdash'])
    cv.snapshot('4 river: short mirror of the band, the sky sheen, dash rows, the boat')
    # ---- near dry grass: tufts
    cv.a[near] = I['ng3']
    cv.mottle(near, I['ng4'], 0.3, 2.0)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(near)
    order = np.argsort(ys)
    for i in order[::6]:
        x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
        L = int(3 + (y - 148) * 0.25 + r.integers(0, 4))
        lean = r.choice([-0.5, -0.25, 0, 0.25, 0.5])
        col = I['ng1'] if r.uniform() < 0.45 else I['ng2']
        for k in range(L):
            X, Y = int(round(x + lean * k)), y - k
            if 0 <= Y < H and 0 <= X < W:
                cv.a[Y, X] = col if k > 0 else I['ng4']
    cv.snapshot('5 near dry grass: tufts, long lit blades')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv = paint()
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/places/out/yakima.png')
    photo = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/yakima_photo.jpg').resize((W, 213), Image.LANCZOS).crop((0, 16, W, 16 + H))
    before = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/yakima_pair_right.png')
    grid([zoom(photo, 2), fit(before, w=640), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)], 1,
         ['photo (Umptanum Ridge water gap, Yakima River, Wikimedia Commons)', 'a place-yakima lane render (evening)', 'painted the Oga way, by hand, 320x180, 2x'],
         title='Yakima canyon').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_yakima.png')
    grid([zoom(cv.pal[a], 2) for _, a in cv.stages], 2, [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='Yakima, stage by stage').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_yakima_stages.png')

251. (manager scratchpad)/p4.png

Write: books/oga/copies/p4_meadow.py
"""Passage copy P4: the grass slope of C-0064 (p.52), 122x60 px.

What he does (looked at in plates/lv/C0064_bg.png, full size):
  - the slope is ONE PLANE with an undulation (C-0095, p.53): the crest facing the sun is a broad
    lemon/yellow-green band; the slope turns away below it through mid green into a dark base band.
    The value turn is the whole form; the grass rides on it.
  - strokes follow the slope's flow: tapered wedges leaning along the fall of the slope, not vertical.
  - contrast lives inside the mark: in the lit band, strokes one step DARKER; in the dark band,
    long blades one step LIGHTER, and a few big dark blade silhouettes near the bottom.
  - the boundaries between bands are not lines: blade tips of the lower band break upward into
    the band above, and lit strokes hang down into the band below.
  - points: blue bellflowers as 1-2 px dots on thin dark stems, a few yellow dots at the right.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(paper=c(0.97, 0.01, 280), lem=c(0.90, 0.17, 108), y1=c(0.83, 0.17, 114), y2=c(0.75, 0.16, 120),
         g1=c(0.64, 0.13, 125), g2=c(0.53, 0.10, 128), g3=c(0.42, 0.075, 125), g4=c(0.32, 0.05, 115), g5=c(0.23, 0.03, 100),
         blue=c(0.58, 0.17, 262), yel=c(0.90, 0.16, 100), trees=c(0.36, 0.07, 130), trees2=c(0.55, 0.12, 122), trees3=c(0.22, 0.03, 140))
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def wedge(cv, x, y, L, ang, idx, where=None):
    """a tapered stroke: 2 px wide at its base (x,y), 1 px for the rest, length L, angle (deg, 0=up)."""
    a = np.radians(ang)
    for k in range(L):
        X = int(round(x + np.sin(a) * k)); Y = int(round(y - np.cos(a) * k))
        for w in ([0, 1] if k < L // 3 else [0]):
            if 0 <= Y < cv.H and 0 <= X + w < cv.W and (where is None or where[Y, X + w]):
                cv.a[Y, X + w] = idx


def paint(seed=4):
    ref = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/copies/p4_ref.png').convert('RGB'); W, H = ref.size
    L = gaussian_filter(rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255)[..., 0], 1.0)
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, bg=I['paper'], seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # masses by hand: the trees behind, the slope, the paper below
    slope_top = lambda x: np.interp(x, [0, 16, 40, 70, 96, 122], [4, 3, 8, 16, 26, 30])
    slope_bot = lambda x: np.interp(x, [0, 10, 60, 122], [46, 54, 56, 58])
    slope = (yy >= slope_top(xx)) & (yy < slope_bot(xx)) & (xx >= 1)
    trees = (yy < slope_top(xx))
    cv.a[trees] = I['trees']
    cv.mottle(trees, I['trees2'], 0.3, 1.2); cv.mottle(trees, I['trees3'], 0.3, 1.5)
    # 1 the plane's value turn: distance below the crest, organised; crest lit band ~ 10-16 px
    d = yy - slope_top(xx)
    depth = slope_bot(xx) - slope_top(xx)
    u = d / depth                                  # 0 at crest, 1 at base
    # his value design modulates the turn a little (organised), e.g. the lit swell at upper left
    Lm = (L - L[slope].mean()) / (L[slope].std() + 1e-6)
    f = u - 0.12 * Lm
    names = ['lem', 'y1', 'y2', 'g1', 'g2', 'g3', 'g4', 'g5']
    t = np.clip(f * 1.15, 0, 0.999) * len(names)
    q = np.clip(np.floor(t), 0, len(names) - 1).astype(int)
    cv.a[slope] = np.array([I[n] for n in names])[q][slope]
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 2)
    cv.snapshot('1 the plane: crest lit, turning through mid to a dark base (value = form)')
    # 2 strokes along the slope's flow; the slope falls to the left-down, so strokes lean right (up-slope)
    base_ang = 28
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(slope)
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=int(len(ys) * 0.16), replace=False):
        x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
        cur = int(cv.a[y, x]); k = [I[n] for n in names].index(cur) if cur in [I[n] for n in names] else 3
        if k <= 2:
            idx = I[names[min(k + 1, 7)]]          # lit band: a darker stroke
            Ls = int(r.integers(2, 4))
        elif k >= 5:
            idx = I[names[max(k - 2, 0)]]          # dark band: a lighter blade
            Ls = int(r.integers(3, 7))
        else:
            idx = I[names[k + (1 if r.uniform() < 0.5 else -1)]]
            Ls = int(r.integers(2, 5))
        wedge(cv, x, y, Ls, base_ang + r.normal(0, 10), idx, where=slope | trees)
    cv.snapshot('2 strokes along the flow, contrast inside the mark')
    # 3 boundary: blade tips of lower bands break upward over the crest line into the trees
    for x in range(1, W):
        y0 = int(slope_top(x))
        if r.uniform() < 0.5:
            wedge(cv, x, y0 + 1, int(r.integers(2, 4)), base_ang - 10 + r.normal(0, 8), I['y1'])
    # 4 big dark blade silhouettes near the base, and stems
    for i in range(14):
        x = int(r.uniform(4, 110)); y = int(slope_bot(x)) - int(r.integers(1, 4))
        wedge(cv, x, y, int(r.integers(8, 16)), r.normal(15, 18), I['g5'])
    for i in range(7):
        x = int(r.uniform(20, 70)); y = int(slope_bot(x)) - 6
        wedge(cv, x, y, int(r.integers(14, 22)), r.normal(10, 10), I['g4'])
    cv.snapshot('3 tips break the crest; dark blade silhouettes at the base')
    # 5 the points: blue bellflowers on stems where his blues are; yellow dots at right
    lab = rgb_to_oklab(np.asarray(ref, float) / 255)
    hue = np.degrees(np.arctan2(lab[..., 2], lab[..., 1])) % 360
    blue = (hue > 240) & (hue < 290) & (np.hypot(lab[..., 1], lab[..., 2]) > 0.08)
    for y, x in zip(*np.nonzero(blue)):
        cv.put(x, y, I['blue'])
    for (x, y) in [(116, 32), (119, 34), (112, 36), (118, 38)]:
        cv.put(x, y, I['yel'])
    cv.snapshot('4 points: bellflowers, yellow dots')
    return cv, ref


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid
    cv, ref = paint()
    cells = [zoom(ref, 6)] + [zoom(cv.pal[a], 6) for _, a in cv.stages] + [zoom(ref, 2), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)]
    grid(cells, 2, ['his, reduced'] + [n for n, _ in cv.stages] + ['his 2x', 'mine 2x'], title='P4 grass slope (C-0064), 6x').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/p4_meadow.png')

Write: books/oga/places/sanjuan.py
"""Place study: the San Juan Islands, a camas meadow under Garry oaks at sunset. 320x180. Hand-designed.
Photo: places/sanjuan_photo.jpg ('Garry Oak Meadow, Victoria BC', Nick Kenrick, CC BY-NC-SA 2.0) -- the
same prairie community as the San Juan balds.

Oga's decisions:
  [7] backlight (C2-2: 'backlight is easier: shapes as silhouettes, the light easy to place'): the sun
      low behind the oaks; everything facing us is in shadow; the light lives only at edges facing
      the sun, in the light's COLOUR (際, C-0772), and in the sky seen through the crowns.
  [7] dusk (C58-1A): warm light, cool shadow, purple between blue and pink; harmonious or it muddies.
  [3] the crowd: oaks as crown masses with sky holes and clumpy edges; the meadow as a plane with the
      camas as a crowd of one mark (a violet spike), small and dense far, big and sparse near.
  [14] the kigo: camas in bloom says 'late April on a bald' by itself; no other detail needed.
"""
import sys
ROOT = __file__.rsplit('/', 2)[0]
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT + '/src')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
from okl import lch_to_lab, oklab_to_rgb, rgb_to_oklab
from copykit import Canvas, majority
import vocab as V

W, H = 320, 180
SUN = (124, 10)


def c(L, C, h):
    return oklab_to_rgb(lch_to_lab(np.array([L, C, np.radians(h)])))


P = dict(
    s0=c(0.99, 0.03, 100), s1=c(0.95, 0.07, 90), s2=c(0.90, 0.09, 75), s3=c(0.84, 0.09, 60), s4=c(0.78, 0.07, 40),
    s5=c(0.73, 0.05, 350), s6=c(0.68, 0.05, 310),
    k1=c(0.24, 0.03, 330), k2=c(0.30, 0.04, 320), k3=c(0.37, 0.045, 340), kw=c(0.46, 0.06, 20),   # crowns: violet darks
    rim1=c(0.80, 0.13, 65), rim2=c(0.70, 0.13, 45),                                               # the light's colour at edges
    tr=c(0.20, 0.02, 330),
    far1=c(0.86, 0.04, 320), far2=c(0.94, 0.05, 80),
    m1=c(0.52, 0.09, 115), m2=c(0.44, 0.08, 125), m3=c(0.36, 0.06, 135), m4=c(0.28, 0.045, 145), mg=c(0.66, 0.11, 105),
    mt=c(0.80, 0.13, 100), mglow=c(0.62, 0.09, 80),
    v1=c(0.72, 0.11, 290), v2=c(0.60, 0.14, 285), v3=c(0.48, 0.14, 280), v4=c(0.40, 0.11, 285), vfar=c(0.60, 0.08, 300),
)
NAMES = list(P); I = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(NAMES)}; PAL = np.array([P[k] for k in NAMES])


def paint(seed=41):
    cv = Canvas(W, H, PAL, seed=seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # ---- 1 the sky: radial bands of the glow around the sun, flat, ragged seams
    d = np.hypot((xx - SUN[0]) * 0.8, (yy - SUN[1]) * 1.3)
    t = np.clip(d / 150, 0, 0.999) * 7
    n = cv.noise(1.0)
    q = np.clip(np.floor(t + 0.35 * n * ((t % 1 < 0.2) | (t % 1 > 0.8))), 0, 6).astype(int)
    cv.a[:] = np.array([I[f's{i}'] for i in range(7)])[q]
    cv.snapshot('1 sky: radial glow bands around the low sun')
    # ---- 2 the oak crowns: masses with sky holes; clumpy lower edges; the light's colour on edges
    crowns = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    for pts in ([(0, 0), (86, 0), (90, 10), (84, 24), (92, 34), (84, 46), (60, 52), (30, 50), (0, 54)],
                [(140, 0), (250, 0), (246, 18), (256, 34), (240, 44), (210, 48), (180, 46), (156, 42), (146, 30), (150, 16)],
                [(296, 20), (310, 12), (320, 10), (320, 50), (300, 50), (290, 40)],
                [(84, 24), (100, 28), (110, 36), (104, 46), (86, 48)],
                [(236, 30), (256, 28), (270, 34), (286, 40), (296, 46), (280, 52), (250, 50)]):
        crowns |= cv.mask_poly(pts)
    # clumpy edges: clumps along the boundary
    edge = crowns & ~np.roll(crowns, 3, 0) | crowns & ~np.roll(crowns, -3, 0) | crowns & ~np.roll(crowns, 3, 1) | crowns & ~np.roll(crowns, -3, 1)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(edge)
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=len(ys) // 5, replace=False):
        rr_ = r.uniform(1.5, 3.2)
        crowns |= (np.hypot((xx - xs[i]) / 1.2, yy - ys[i]) < rr_)
    cv.a[crowns] = I['k2']
    cv.mottle(crowns, I['k1'], 0.4, 2.5)
    cv.mottle(crowns, I['k3'], 0.2, 2.0)
    # sky holes through the crowns, more toward the sun and the crown edges
    near_sun = np.exp(-d / 90)
    holes = crowns & (cv.noise(0.7) > 1.6 - 1.2 * near_sun)
    cv.a[holes] = q[holes] + I['s0']
    # the light's colour where the crown's edge faces the sun (際): 1-px rim, and warm inner glow
    sky = ~crowns
    to_sun_x = np.sign(SUN[0] - xx); to_sun_y = np.sign(SUN[1] - yy)
    nb = np.roll(np.roll(sky, -to_sun_y.astype(int)[0, 0], 0), 0, 1)
    rim = crowns & (np.roll(sky, 1, 0) | np.roll(sky, -1, 1) * (xx < SUN[0]) | np.roll(sky, 1, 1) * (xx > SUN[0])) & (d < 130)
    cv.a[rim & (d < 70)] = I['rim1']
    cv.a[rim & (d >= 70)] = I['rim2']
    cv.a[crowns & ~rim & (d < 60) & (cv.noise(1.5) > 0.8)] = I['kw']
    cv.snapshot('2 oak crowns: violet masses, sky holes, rims of the light at edges facing the sun')
    # ---- 3 trunks: dark verticals, a warm edge on the sun side
    for (x, y0, y1, lean) in [(18, 50, 64, 0.0), (40, 48, 64, 0.02), (62, 48, 64, -0.02), (74, 46, 63, 0.0), (96, 44, 63, 0.05),
                              (160, 40, 62, 0.0), (172, 42, 62, -0.03), (190, 44, 62, 0.02), (214, 44, 62, 0.0), (232, 44, 62, 0.03),
                              (262, 48, 62, 0.0), (300, 46, 62, 0.0)]:
        for y in range(y0, y1):
            X = int(round(x + lean * (y - y0)))
            cv.put(X, y, I['tr']); cv.put(X + 1, y, I['tr'])
            side = X - 1 if X > SUN[0] else X + 2
            if r.uniform() < 0.6:
                cv.put(side, y, I['rim2'])
    cv.snapshot('3 trunks, warm edge on the sun side')
    # ---- 4 the far strip: the bright field/water beyond, the brightest thing under the sun
    strip = (yy >= 55) & (yy < 63) & ~crowns
    cv.a[strip] = I['far1']
    cv.a[strip & (np.abs(xx - SUN[0]) < 60) & (yy >= 57) & (yy < 60)] = I['far2']
    # ---- 5 the meadow: one plane, backlit: the far meadow glows toward the sun; nearer it darkens
    meadow = yy >= 62
    u = (yy - 62) / (H - 62)
    glow = np.exp(-np.abs(xx - SUN[0]) / 90) * (1 - u) ** 2
    f = u * 3.2 - glow * 1.6 + 0.25 * cv.noise(2.0)
    mq = np.clip(np.floor(f + 1), 0, 4).astype(int)
    cv.a[meadow] = np.array([I['mglow'], I['m1'], I['m2'], I['m3'], I['m4']])[mq][meadow]
    cv.a = majority(cv.a, 1)
    cv.snapshot('4 far strip; meadow plane: glow far toward the sun, darkening near')
    # grass blades: backlit, lit tips (the light's colour inside the mark), longer near
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(meadow)
    for i in r.choice(len(ys), size=int(len(ys) * 0.09), replace=False):
        x, y = xs[i], ys[i]
        uu = (y - 62) / (H - 62)
        L = 1 + int(uu * 9 * r.uniform(0.5, 1.2))
        lean = r.choice([-0.3, -0.15, 0, 0.15, 0.3])
        body = I['m3'] if uu > 0.4 else I['m1']
        for k in range(L):
            cv.put(x + lean * k, y - k, body)
        cv.put(x + lean * (L - 1), y - L + 1, I['mt'] if r.uniform() < 0.6 else I['mg'])
    cv.snapshot('5 grass: backlit blades, lit tips')
    # ---- 6 the camas: the crowd of one mark, dense+tiny far, big+sparse near; sun-side top lit
    for band_y0, band_y1, n_, size in [(62, 72, 900, 0), (72, 90, 420, 1), (90, 120, 200, 2), (120, 150, 90, 3), (150, 180, 45, 4)]:
        for k in range(n_):
            x = r.uniform(0, W); y = r.uniform(band_y0, band_y1)
            if size == 0:
                cv.put(x, y, I['vfar'] if r.uniform() < 0.7 else I['v1']); continue
            h_ = size + 1 + r.integers(0, 2)
            cv.line([(x, y + h_ + size), (x, y)], I['m3'])                    # stem
            for j in range(h_):
                for w_ in range(-(size // 2), size // 2 + 1):
                    cv.put(x + w_, y + j, I['v3'] if j > h_ // 2 else I['v2'])
            cv.put(x, y - 1, I['v1'])                                          # lit top toward the sun
            if size >= 3:
                cv.put(x + (1 if x < SUN[0] else -1), y, I['v1'])
                cv.put(x - (size // 2), y + h_ - 1, I['v4'])
    cv.snapshot('6 camas: one mark, tiny and dense far, big and sparse near, lit tops')
    return cv


if __name__ == '__main__':
    from sheet import zoom, grid, fit
    cv = paint()
    Image.fromarray((cv.rgb() * 255 + .5).astype(np.uint8)).save(f'{ROOT}/places/out/sanjuan.png')
    photo = Image.open(f'{ROOT}/places/sanjuan_crop.png')
    grid([zoom(photo, 2), zoom(cv.rgb(), 2)], 1,
         ['photo (Garry oak meadow, Nick Kenrick, CC BY-NC-SA 2.0)', 'painted the Oga way, by hand, 320x180, 2x'],
         title='San Juan: camas under Garry oaks at sunset').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_sanjuan.png')
    grid([zoom(cv.pal[a], 2) for _, a in cv.stages], 2, [n for n, _ in cv.stages], title='San Juan, stage by stage').save(f'{ROOT}/sheets/place_sanjuan_stages.png')
